#### V07-072 Topic Modelling: bubble opacity shows how concentrated a topic is across Colour by values (#188)

- Commits: 3588b72f
- Area: Topic Modelling
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: With Colour by, the fused colour shows the top two over-represented values but not whether a topic is concentrated or spread across many values (Chao's decision).
- What changed:
  - With Colour by active (single-corpus runs only, `corpusCount <= 1 && colorScheme`), resting opacity = 0.25 + 0.55 × √C, where C = 1 − Pielou's evenness J, J = H / ln K, H = −Σ pᵢ ln pᵢ, pᵢ = (topic count for value i / document count of value i) normalised over all K values (zero shares add nothing). One value (or K < 2) gives C = 1, opacity 0.8; an even spread gives 0.25. If the weights total 0, opacity falls back to 0.6.
  - Without Colour by, all bubbles use 0.6.
  - Hover lifts a bubble to 0.92 (above the range; CSS group-hover also 0.92). Selection no longer raises opacity (was 0.78); it is shown by the green outline (`#16a34a`, width 2).
  - Exported SVG uses the same resting opacity.
  - Colour by tooltip adds: "A more solid bubble is concentrated in few values; a paler one is spread evenly."
  - Tutorial section "What bubble opacity shows" (`help-topic-modeling-color-by-opacity`) documents the formula with a worked example (counts 60, 58, 10 give C ≈ 0.17, opacity ≈ 0.48; 60, 58, 57 give ≈ 0.26).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A topic whose documents all have one value renders at fill-opacity 0.8.
  - A topic spread evenly (relative to value sizes) renders at 0.25.
  - Hover shows 0.92; a selected, not hovered bubble keeps its resting opacity with a green ring.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/features/views/topic-modeling/components/results/topicModelingGraph.ts` (`TOPIC_OPACITY_MIN/MAX/DEFAULT/HOVER`, `topicValueConcentration`, `topicColorSchemeOpacity`, `TopicBubbleModel.fillOpacity`), `TopicModelingFlowChart.tsx` (`TopicBubbleNode`, `TopicExportSvg`), `panels/TopicModelingResultsPanel.tsx`, `public/tutorials/topic-modeling.md`.
- Tests: `topicModelingGraph.test.ts` ("sets opacity from how evenly a topic spreads across the values"), `TopicModelingFlowChart.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: Frontend-only maths from `scheme.topicCounts` and `group.documentCount`; keep the exact formula and constants so help text stays true. Checked in WebKit.

#### V07-073 Topic Modelling: no bubble fully hidden behind another (#189)

- Commits: 73005897
- Area: Topic Modelling
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: Chao's screenshot showed T7 fully covering T6 and T9 covering T2, labels included. Positions were the 2D projection scaled to the canvas with no overlap handling, drawn in topic order. Decision: partial overlap is allowed and meaningful, a huge bubble should not push everything away, but no bubble may be completely hidden.
- What changed:
  - A deterministic collision relaxation runs on the projected positions. For every pair, required distance = max(r_large + 0.35 × r_small, 26 px), so the smaller bubble's centre (where its label is) stays outside the larger one and two labels are never closer than 26 px.
  - Moves are split by area weights (the smaller bubble moves most). 240 anchored rounds with a pull back to the projected spot of 0.04 that fades linearly to 0, then up to 3,000 separation-only passes until the worst shortfall < 0.1 px. Coincident bubbles split along a fixed golden-angle direction derived from their ids.
  - The result is cached (keyed by id, x, y, radius of every bubble) so hover and selection do not recompute it.
  - Draw order: larger bubbles first (by `total_size` desc, then id), so smaller bubbles are on top.
  - Chart, lasso and exported SVG share the relaxed positions. Radius is unchanged: 10 + 40 × √(size / max size).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - For every pair of visible bubbles, centre distance ≥ max(r_large + 0.35 r_small, 26).
  - Two bubbles at the same projected point are separated identically on every run.
  - An isolated bubble keeps its projected position.
  - A 200-topic crowded layout finishes (about 0.1 s) with nothing hidden.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/features/views/topic-modeling/components/results/topicModelingGraph.ts` (`relaxTopicPositions`, `relaxedPositionsFor` module-level cache, `buildTopicBubbleModels` draw order). Tutorial paragraph in `topic-modeling.md`.
- Tests: `topicModelingGraph.test.ts` ("pulls a covered bubble out until its centre is visible, moving the smaller one more", "splits bubbles at exactly the same spot the same way every time", "leaves no bubble hidden in a crowded layout").
- v0.8 notes: The diff does not clamp relaxed positions to the canvas; unclear whether bubbles can be pushed outside `TOPIC_GRAPH_WIDTH/HEIGHT` (not verified). Keep determinism so lasso and export match the screen. Checked in WebKit.

#### V07-093 Topic coverage columns: left out of analysis metadata; refused where they break (#200)

- Commits: 9b22ca51
- Area: Concordance, Quotation, Trends, Topic Modelling, Annotation, Data Builder, Data Editor, Data View, Export
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: Concordance Run All on a block detached from Topic Modelling with per-document coverage failed: `InvalidOperationError: cannot cast List type ... to Extension(org.ldaca.wordflow.topic_coverage.v1 ...)`. Run All workers carried other columns as metadata by copying them into Python lists and casting back, which the topic-coverage extension type (a fixed-size array of `{topic_id, coverage}` structs) cannot survive. Agreed approach (Chao): treat it as not supported yet; leave it out of metadata and stop it being selected where it would break.
- What changed:
  - One shared backend rule (`shared/unsupported_columns.py`): `is_unsupported_metadata_dtype` matches the extension and its physical storage; `supported_metadata_columns`, `without_unsupported_columns`, `require_supported_columns`, and the error `"<col>" holds topic coverage values, which cannot be used <use> yet. Choose another column.` (uses: "with a type change", "as text", "as a join key", "to group rows", "with this summary").
  - Analyses: Concordance and Quotation (Preview and Run All), Trends Results, and Topic Modelling colours leave the column out of carried/shown/grouped metadata; Trends refuses it as time axis or group before execution; frontend metadata pickers (Concordance, Annotation Manual/Preview/Review, Trends group-by) omit it.
  - Data Builder and Data Editor: tools reading a column as text, a key, a group, an ordered summary, or a type change refuse it; summaries `first`, `last`, `count_distinct` still accept it. Column pickers in Data Builder tools other than Filter no longer list it. Carrying it through works: Filter on another column, Filter by topic threshold, Sample, Stack, Join on another key, Deduplicate, Duplicate, Rename, Delete.
  - Data View: Sort disabled with tooltip "Topic coverage columns can't be sorted yet."; Change data type disabled with "Topic coverage columns can't change type yet."; the column tools menu offers only Duplicate.
  - Filter by topic threshold on a detached block works again (it read the extension instead of its stored values; failed with `expected Array datatype`).
  - CSV and Excel exports write it as JSON text of its topic/coverage pairs instead of crashing; Parquet keeps it.
  - Architecture docs updated (`docs/architecture/backend/analysis-data-flow.md`, `frontend/state-and-data-flow.md`).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Concordance Run All on a detached per-document coverage block succeeds, and its results have no TOPIC_coverage column.
  - Trends with the coverage column as group is refused with the message above.
  - Join on the coverage column is refused "as a join key".
  - Data View Sort button on the coverage column is disabled with its tooltip.
  - Filter "topic ≥ threshold" on a detached block returns rows above the threshold.
  - CSV export shows readable JSON in that column.
- Implementation: backend `shared/unsupported_columns.py` (new), `analysis/concordance_core.py`, `analysis/quotation_core.py`, `analysis/sequential_core.py`, `services/analysis_preparation_registry.py`, `services/analysis_results.py`, `services/data_block_exports.py` (extension columns written from `.ext.storage()`), `services/node_operations.py` (checks in each plan builder; `_topic_coverage_expression(condition, dtype)` uses `.ext.storage()` before `.arr.eval`), `workers/concordance.py`, `workers/quotation.py`. Frontend `lib/arrow/semanticTypes.ts` (`isSupportedColumnField`), `workspace/data-view/components/WorkspaceColumnHeader.tsx` (`isTopicCoverage`), `WorkspaceTable.tsx`, `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts`, `preprocessing/DataPreprocessingFeature.tsx` (`fieldPredicate`), `join/hooks/useJoinSubTab.ts`, `sequential-analysis/SequentialAnalysisFeature.tsx`, `concordance/hooks/useConcordanceMetadataColumns.ts`, annotation panels and `RunAllReviewTable.tsx`, `analysisNavigation.ts` (formatting only).
- Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_topic_coverage_columns.py` (43 tests with real Parquet data and the extension loaded; includes one reproducing the original error), `useConcordanceMetadataColumns.test.ts`, `WorkspaceTable.test.tsx` ("offers topic coverage only the column tools that can use it"), `arrowTable.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: The topic coverage type is a Polars extension type (`org.ldaca.wordflow.topic_coverage.v1`); `.ext.storage()` is Polars-specific. In DuckDB it would be a `STRUCT(topic_id BIGINT, coverage DOUBLE)[]` (LIST or ARRAY) column, which DuckDB handles natively; the v0.7 restrictions may not all be needed, but the Filter-by-threshold and readable CSV/Excel export behaviour must be preserved.

#### V07-136 Charts: plain scroll scrolls the pane; Cmd (Ctrl) + scroll zooms (#215)

- Commits: 914e6a80
- Area: Charts (shared `EChartsView`, Topic Modelling graph, Project Graph)
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: The Topic Modelling bubble chart zoomed on a plain scroll and swallowed it, so the pane could not scroll with the pointer over the large chart. Chao asked for one rule in every chart: plain scrolling never zooms, and Cmd + scroll zooms. Checking Trends in WebKit found a second bug: since #213 turned wheel zoom off, a plain scroll over the Trends chart did nothing at all, because ECharts cancels every wheel event over an inside dataZoom before it checks whether any wheel behaviour is on.
- What changed:
  - New `frontend/src/lib/chartZoom.ts`: `CHART_ZOOM_KEY` ('Meta' on Apple platforms, 'Control' elsewhere), `ECHARTS_WHEEL_ZOOM_MODIFIER` ('meta' or 'ctrl'), `isChartZoomWheel(event)`.
  - Topic Modelling graph: `zoomOnScroll={false}`, `panOnScroll={false}`, `zoomActivationKeyCode={CHART_ZOOM_KEY}`, pinch still zooms. React Flow drops the zoom key when page scrolling is allowed, so `preventScrolling` follows the key through `useKeyPress(CHART_ZOOM_KEY)`.
  - `EChartsView` (Trends and the Concordance dispersion chart): `zoomOnMouseWheel: ECHARTS_WHEEL_ZOOM_MODIFIER` (ECharts checks `event[modifier + 'Key']`, so 'meta' works although its types list only shift, ctrl and alt). A capture-phase wheel listener on the plot element stops plain wheel events before ECharts sees them, so the pane scrolls. Trackpad pinch does not zoom ECharts charts.
  - Project Graph: unchanged behaviour (scroll pans, pinch or Cmd + scroll zooms, issue 194; Shift + scroll pans sideways); the zoom key is now named from the same module.
  - Help: Trends, Topic Modelling and Concordance pages describe Cmd (Ctrl) + scroll.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Over the Topic Modelling graph, Trends chart or Concordance dispersion chart, a plain wheel or two-finger scroll scrolls the pane and leaves the chart unchanged.
  - With Cmd (macOS) or Ctrl (Windows, Linux) held, the same scroll zooms the chart and the pane does not move.
  - A trackpad pinch zooms the Topic Modelling graph and the Project Graph.
  - The Project Graph still pans on a plain scroll and zooms on Cmd + scroll.
- Implementation: `lib/chartZoom.ts`; `features/views/topic-modeling/components/results/TopicModelingFlowChart.tsx`; `features/views/common/components/EChartsView.tsx` (`keepPageScroll`); `features/workspace/graph-view/components/WorkspaceGraphFeature.tsx`; help `tutorials/sequential-analysis.md`, `topic-modeling.md`, `concordance.md`.
- Tests: `EChartsView.test.tsx` ("keeps plain wheel events away from ECharts so the pane scrolls", "zooms on the wheel only with Ctrl or Cmd held ..."); `TopicModelingFlowChart.test.tsx` ("lets the pane scroll and zooms only with the zoom key held"); `WorkspaceGraphFeature.test.tsx` (zoom key); manual Playwright WebKit run measuring pane scroll and chart state for plain and Cmd scrolls.
- v0.8 notes: an ECharts inside dataZoom cancels every wheel event over the chart, whatever its settings; filter wheel events before ECharts if the page must scroll. Keep one zoom key for all charts.

#### V07-154 Topic Modelling: seed each sample with its percentage (#237)

- Commits: 2821cccd
- Area: Topic Modelling
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Each Data Block's sample was seeded with the Seed field plus the Data Block's position (`random_seed + index`), so the same percentage picked different documents when the Seed changed or the Data Block moved.
- What changed:
  - `sample_seed_for_fraction(fraction)` in `workers/topic_pipeline.py` returns `round(fraction * 100)`; `_sample_corpora_for_topic_modeling` uses it and no longer takes `random_seed`.
  - The Seed field now controls only the model (dimensionality reduction and clustering). 100% is not sampled.
  - Help: `tutorials/topic-modeling.md`, Step 2 and Seed.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A 20% sample uses seed 20 in either Data Block, whatever the Seed field; 29% uses 29 (not 28 from float truncation).
  - Re-running an existing Tab with a sample below 100% picks a different sample than before this change.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/workers/topic_pipeline.py`, `workers/topic_modeling.py`.
- Tests: `test_topic_modeling_worker.py` (`test_sample_seed_is_the_whole_number_percentage`, `test_each_sample_is_seeded_by_its_percentage_not_its_position`).
- v0.8 notes: seed a Topic Modelling sample with its whole-number percentage, independent of the model seed.

### Quotation

#### V07-001 Quotation Run All fails validation, no "Add to Workspace" (#110)

- Commits: aa22e185
- Area: Quotation
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: Running Quotation "Run All" failed in the Tasks centre with `ValidationError ... QuotationRunAllWorkerResult source.source_document_count Field required`. The vNext contract cutover (3693d82a, first shipped in v0.7.7) made `source_document_count` required on `RunAllSourceDescriptor`; the Concordance worker was updated but the Quotation worker was not. With no successful Run All Result, `runAllSource` stays null and the tab falls back to the Preview "Search Results" panel, which has no "Add to Workspace" button.
- What changed: The Quotation Run All worker now reports `source_document_count` (number of all source documents, `len(node_corpus)`) next to `document_count` (documents with at least one quotation, `quote_df.height`) and `match_count`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Quotation Run All completes; the Review panel shows with an "Add to Workspace" button.
  - The worker result validates against `QuotationRunAllWorkerResult`.
  - For a corpus with a document containing no quotations, `source_document_count` > `document_count`.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/workers/quotation.py` `run_quotation_run_all` (source descriptor dict).
- Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_quotation_run_all_worker.py` now validates the output dict against the worker result model and includes a document with no quotations.
- v0.8 notes: Any Run All style worker contract must carry both the total source document count and the matched document count; validate worker outputs against the result model in tests (the old test only checked selected keys).

#### V07-002 Quote Extraction cells bounded, clamped, with full-text hover tooltip (#111, #112)

- Commits: fcad731d, 844b9750, c67c040e
- Area: Quotation
- Type: bug fix (layout) + feature (tooltip)
- Problem / reason: The result table frame used `min-w-max`, so long quotation context made the Quote Extraction column extremely wide (#111). Users needed a way to read the full context of a clamped cell (#112). Review feedback: 32rem too narrow; tooltip blended into the table (same surface colour); tooltip opened only intermittently.
- What changed:
  - Quote Extraction (document pseudo-column) cells wrap inside a fixed width of 42rem (first 32rem in fcad731d, widened about 30% in 844b9750) and are clamped to 3 lines with an ellipsis (`line-clamp-3`, `whitespace-normal break-words`). Metadata columns unchanged.
  - Hovering a cell shows a tooltip with the complete highlighted context (same speaker/verb/quote badges as the cell), only when the clamp actually hides content (`scrollHeight > clientHeight + 1`). Short cells show no tooltip, by design. Tooltip open delay 300ms.
  - Tooltip styling: side bottom, align start, `sideOffset` 8px, `alignOffset` 48px (shifted right so it does not read as the next row), info-tinted background (`bg-info-background`), 2px focus-coloured border (`border-2 border-focus`), large shadow (`--vscode-shadow-lg`), rounded, max width `max-w-2xl`, an arrow (14x7, `fill-focus`) pointing back at the cell. Content scrolls inside an inner wrapper capped at 60vh so the bubble does not clip its arrow. All colours are theme tokens (light and dark).
  - Intermittent tooltip fix: hovering a highlighted segment lifts hover state into `QuotationFeature`, which re-renders the node block and rebuilds column defs. TanStack `flexRender` mounts a function `cell` as a React component, so each rebuilt closure was a new component type, remounting every body cell and resetting the Radix tooltip's open state and delay timer. Body cells now call a local `renderCell(columnName, row, rowId)` directly (headers still use `flexRender`). Side effect: highlight spans stop flickering on hover.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A long Quote Extraction cell is 42rem wide and shows at most 3 lines.
  - Hovering a clamped cell for about 300ms opens a tinted, outlined tooltip with the full context and an arrow; a cell whose content fits shows no tooltip.
  - Moving the pointer across highlighted words inside the cell does not close or restart the tooltip.
  - Re-rendering the block with new hover state keeps the same cell DOM node (no remount).
- Implementation: new `frontend/src/features/views/quotation/components/QuotationClampedCell.tsx` (Radix tooltip from `@/components/ui/tooltip` plus `TooltipPrimitive.Arrow` from `radix-ui`, controlled `open` gated by `isClamped()`); `QuotationNodeBlock.tsx` (`renderCell`, body cells no longer through `flexRender`).
- Tests: `quotation/components/__tests__/QuotationClampedCell.test.tsx` (width/clamp classes, tooltip only when clamped, none when content fits); `__tests__/QuotationNodeBlock.test.tsx` (regression: cell DOM node survives hover-state re-render).
- v0.8 notes: Keep the fixed-width 3-line clamp and clamp-gated tooltip. In any TanStack table whose column defs capture changing state, do not render stateful cells through `flexRender` with inline closures (remount hazard); call a render function directly or memoise the column defs.

#### V07-003 Row details opens scrolled to the extracted quote (#113)

- Commits: 75a950f8
- Area: Quotation (shared Row details panel)
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: The Row details "Document" box always opened at the top of the full text, so users had to scroll and search for the quotation.
- What changed: Detail renderers can mark one element with `data-row-detail-anchor`. The shared `RowDetailPanel` scrolls only the Document box (not the whole dialog) so the anchor sits near the top with a 48px context margin above it, on open and on previous/next row navigation. Without an anchor the box scrolls to the top. Summary fields above stay in view. Quotation marks its first highlighted segment, so in "Documents" paging mode a document with several quotes opens at the first one. Other users of the panel (Concordance) are unaffected until they add an anchor.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Opening Row details for a quotation row shows the Document box scrolled so the first highlighted quote is about 48px below the box top.
  - Navigating to the next/previous row re-applies the scroll.
  - The dialog's summary fields remain at the top of the dialog.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/features/views/common/components/RowDetailPanel.tsx` (`scrollDocumentToAnchor`, `DOCUMENT_ANCHOR_MARGIN_PX = 48`, `data-testid="row-detail-document"`); the Document box is tracked with a state-backed callback ref (`useState` + `ref={setDocumentBox}`) because Radix mounts dialog content one render after `open` flips, so a plain `useRef` is still null when the effect first runs. `quotation/components/quotationDetailText.tsx` `renderQuotationDetailText` adds `data-row-detail-anchor` to the first segment with types.
- Tests: `common/components/__tests__/RowDetailPanel.test.tsx` (scroll offset).
- v0.8 notes: Keep the generic anchor contract so other analyses (Concordance) can opt in. Radix dialog content mount timing: use a callback/state ref, not a plain ref, for post-open effects.

#### V07-060 Quotation: explain the quote types (#174)

- Commits: 9ff1074c (follow-up efea30af puts the explanation on its own smaller line, outside this range)
- Area: Quotation, Row Details, Docs
- Type: feature / docs
- Problem / reason: User feedback: Quote Type values (QCQSV, CSV, SVC, AccordingTo, Heuristic) were shown raw and unexplained.
- What changed: Row Details "Quote Type" shows "<code>: <Description>". Letter codes list parts in sentence order: Q quotation mark, C content, V verb, S speaker; C becomes "the quote in quotation marks" when the code contains Q, else "the quote (reported speech)", joined with ", then ". Specials: QCQ "a quote in quotation marks that continues the previous quote, whose speaker it takes"; AccordingTo 'attributed with "according to"'; Heuristic "text between quotation marks found by a fallback rule; the speaker and verb are the nearest ones and may be missing". Unknown codes show raw. Tutorial adds a quote-type legend with examples and notes that exported speaker, verb and quote columns carry start and end offsets.

  - "QCQVS" shows "QCQVS: The quote in quotation marks, then the verb, then the speaker".
  - "SVC" shows "SVC: The speaker, then the verb, then the quote (reported speech)".
  - An unknown code such as "XYZ" shows "XYZ".
- Implementation: new `frontend/src/features/views/quotation/quoteTypes.ts` (`describeQuoteType`, `formatQuoteType`); `quotationRowDetail.ts`; `tutorials/quotation.md`.
- Tests: `frontend/src/features/views/quotation/__tests__/quoteTypes.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: Frontend-only mapping; keep it in sync with the quotation extractor's codes.

#### V07-063 Quotation: Date and Datetime columns shown as dates (#177)

- Commits: e09712a4
- Area: Quotation, Row Details
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: Quotation decodes Arrow rows with its own native decoder (`nativeRows` in `quotationArrowPage.ts`) so Int64 character offsets stay exact (bigint). That decoder skipped the shared Date/Timestamp formatting from #165, so a source Date showed as epoch milliseconds (2020-01-31 as `1580428800000`) in Row Details and in the results table metadata columns, in both Preview and Run All Review. Also a List<Int64> or Struct metadata column threw in Row Details because `JSON.stringify` cannot write bigint. Display only: Add to Project and Export were already correct.
- What changed: Top-level Date columns show as `YYYY-MM-DD`; Timestamp columns show as a UTC ISO string (`new Date(ms).toISOString()`). Int64 offsets stay native bigint. Row Details writes nested bigint values as decimal strings.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A Quotation source Date column value 2020-01-31 shows "2020-01-31" in the results table and Row Details (Preview and Run All).
  - A Datetime shows as an ISO timestamp.
  - Opening Row Details for a row with a List<Int64> metadata column does not throw and shows the numbers.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/lib/arrow/arrowTable.ts` new exported `formatArrowTemporalValue(value, type)` (shared with `normalizeArrowValue`); `features/views/quotation/quotationArrowPage.ts` `nativeRows` applies it to schema fields where `DataType.isDate || DataType.isTimestamp`; `RowDetailPanel.tsx` `formatMetadataValue` uses a `JSON.stringify` replacer converting bigint to string.
- Tests: `RowDetailPanel.test.tsx` ("shows nested big integers and leaves missing values empty"), `quotationArrowPage.test.ts` ("shows source Date and Timestamp columns as dates, keeping Int64 offsets exact").
- v0.8 notes: Any decoder that keeps Int64 as bigint for exact offsets must still format temporal columns. Timestamps display in UTC ISO form here (not local time). If v0.8 still uses Arrow from DuckDB, DATE arrives as Date32 (days) and TIMESTAMP may arrive with a different unit; format both.

#### V07-122 AI Annotation in plain words (#205 item 16)

- Commits: 2a022a7b
- Area: Annotation AI settings, provider dialog, inference settings per provider, Run settings, review tables
- Type: wording + layout change
- Problem / reason: codebook entries called "codes" and "classes"; required Provider and Model hidden under "Advanced settings"; "API Key (optional)" contradicted "required before use"; provider jargon ("native thinking controls", "sampling", "Chat Completions contract", "upstream provider"); "Thinking" vs "Reasoning"; Run settings jargon.
- What changed (exact):
  - "code" everywhere: "Select class" to "Select code"; "Max examples per class" to "Max examples per code"; "Not a Codebook class; treated as empty" to "Not a code in the Codebook, so treated as empty" (AI preview, results panel, current value item, Run review table).
  - Provider and Model pickers are always in view above the collapsible; the collapsible trigger now reads "Advanced settings" and holds only the prompt, provider settings and Run settings. (Before, the collapsed trigger showed the provider and model summary with "No provider selected" / "No model selected".) Note: the agreed decision said "next to Run"; the implementation places them at the top of the AI settings, always visible.
  - Provider dialog: "Must implement OpenAI's Chat Completions API." to "The server must accept OpenAI-style chat requests (the Chat Completions API)."; key hint "You can save this provider now, but an API key is required before use." to "You can save the provider now and add the key later in Settings; it is needed before you can list models or run Annotation."; custom: "Leave blank when the Custom endpoint does not require authentication." to "Leave blank if your server does not need one." The API key stays optional.
  - "Reasoning" to "Thinking" (label, "Toggle thinking", "Thinking effort") for OpenAI, OpenRouter and Custom. Descriptions: Anthropic and Google thinking "Let the model think step by step before it answers. Slower, but often more accurate."; OpenAI/OpenRouter "Let the model think step by step before it answers, where the model supports it. Slower, but often more accurate."; Custom "Let the model think step by step before it answers. Use only if your model supports it."
  - Temperature descriptions: OpenAI "Lower values give more consistent answers; higher values more varied ones. Not used while Thinking is on."; Google and OpenRouter "…Not every model uses it."; Custom "…Use only if your model supports it."
  - Run settings: "These controls belong to Wordflow and apply to every provider." to "These settings apply to every provider."; legend "Run processing" to "Which rows to annotate"; "Reprocess all rows" to "Annotate all rows again"; "Fill missing only" to "Only rows without an annotation"; explanation "Annotating all rows again replaces the annotation column; the other choice keeps the annotations you already have."; "Batch size" to "Rows per request", help "How many rows go to the AI model in each request (default 20, up to 100)."; "Max retries per batch" to "Retries if a request fails", help "How many more times to try a request that fails (default 2, so 3 tries in all). 0 means no retries."
  - Help annotation.md updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable): Provider and Model are visible without opening Advanced settings; all providers show a "Thinking" control; Run settings show "Rows per request" (default 20, max 100) and "Retries if a request fails" (default 2).
- Implementation: `annotation/components/AnnotationAiSettings.tsx`, `AddAnnotationProviderDialog.tsx`, `AnnotationAiPreviewPanel.tsx`, `AnnotationExampleSamplingControls.tsx`, `AnnotationResultsPanel.tsx`, `CurrentAnnotationValueItem.tsx`, `inference-settings/*.tsx`, `common/components/RunAllReviewTable.tsx`.
- Tests: `AddAnnotationProviderDialog.test.tsx`, `AnnotationAiSettings.test.tsx`, `AnnotationExampleSamplingControls.test.tsx`, `AnnotationInferenceSettings.test.tsx`, `TopicModelingParameterPanel.test.tsx` (label update).
- v0.8 notes: internal field ids keep "class" and "batch" (for example `annotation-ai-max-examples-per-class`, `annotation-ai-batch-size`); only labels change.

### Data Loader and files

#### V07-019 Add a folder of texts as one Data Block; shared document rule for folders and ZIPs; skipped-files report (#134)

- Commits: 6b3fc68f, e6102316
- Area: Data Loader
- Type: feature + behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Folders could be uploaded recursively but not loaded, although a ZIP of the same texts could. ZIP loading made a row from every member that decoded as UTF-8, whatever the extension, so a metadata CSV became one document holding the whole table; non-UTF-8 files were skipped silently. The first version of the skip report was a separate toast that the success toast stacked on top of and hid.
- What changed:
  - Folder rows in the File Tree get a "+" icon button (aria-label "Add folder <name> as a Data Block"; title "Add the text files in this folder as one Data Block", or "Load a project to add this folder as a Data Block" when no project is loaded, then disabled). It opens the preview dialog titled "Add Folder: <name>" with description "Every .txt, .text, .md, .rst and .log file in this folder and its subfolders becomes one row. Other files (for example PDF, Word, CSV) are skipped and listed after adding. Inspect the preview, then confirm." Confirm creates one document Data Block with columns `file_path`, `base_name`, `extension`, `document` (same reader as ZIP).
  - Document rule, shared by folders and ZIPs: only `.txt`, `.text`, `.md`, `.rst`, `.log` files become rows (whole file per row); files with other or no extension are skipped (reported as "(no extension)"); non-UTF-8 text is skipped and reported; hidden and OS files (any path part starting with "." such as `.DS_Store`, `.git`, `._*`, or `__MACOSX`) are ignored without being reported. Folder walks are recursive, in path order, never follow symlinks, junctions or reparse points; `file_path` is relative to the chosen folder.
  - Limits: a folder's text files together share the single-file source size limit (error "Folder is too large for node ingestion" / "Folder is too large to preview", HTTP 413); at most 100,000 files ("Folder contains too many files to load").
  - API: node resource gets `skipped_files: [{extension, reason: "unsupported_type" | "not_utf8", count}] | null`, largest groups first.
  - After adding, the success toast "<name> added to project." carries the skip report as its description, shown for 10 seconds, e.g. "50 files skipped while loading: pdf - 30, xlsx - 10, docx - 5, csv - 4, exe - 1"; non-UTF-8 entries show as "txt (not UTF-8) - N". (e6102316 moved it from a separate toast into the success toast's description.)
  - Not in scope: joining a metadata CSV onto documents (load it separately and Join on `base_name`).
  - Tutorial `frontend/public/tutorials/data-loader.md` updated. (The folder add is extended with Texts/Tables modes in #136.)
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Adding a folder containing a.txt, b.md, c.pdf, d.csv, .DS_Store and a subfolder with e.txt creates 3 rows (a, b, sub/e) and a toast description "2 files skipped while loading: csv - 1, pdf - 1" (order by count; ties order not specified in the formatter).
  - A ZIP with a metadata.csv no longer produces a row for it.
  - A symlink inside the folder is ignored.
  - A folder whose text files exceed the source size limit returns 413.
- Implementation: backend `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py` (`DOCUMENT_EXTENSIONS`, `MAX_DIRECTORY_DOCUMENTS`, `SkippedDocumentFiles`, `_DocumentCollector`, `_read_zip_documents`, `_read_directory_documents`, `read_documents`, `DirectoryTooLargeError`, uses `os.scandir` + `is_link_or_reparse`), `services/user_files.py` (`read_path(..., allow_directory=True)` admits real no-follow folders), `services/file_preview.py`, `services/nodes.py`, `models/workspace.py` (`SkippedFileCount`); frontend `data-loader/components/FileTree.tsx`, `AddFilePanel.tsx` (`isFolder`), `DataLoaderFeature.tsx` (`notify` with description, duration 10000ms when described), `hooks/useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.ts`, `features/workspace/common/skippedFiles.ts` (`describeSkippedFiles`), `useWorkspaceGraphMutations.ts`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/integration/test_folder_data_block.py` (upload, preview, create end to end), `backend/tests/unit/test_data_loading.py` (rules, links, size limit); frontend `FileTree.test.tsx`, `features/workspace/common/__tests__/skippedFiles.test.ts`, `hooks/__tests__/useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.test.tsx` (single toast with description).
- v0.8 notes: The document table is built with Polars from Python-collected records; DuckDB could do the same via a Python walk (DuckDB `read_text` would not apply the extension/UTF-8/skip rules). Preserve the skip report and the no-link-follow rule (security).

#### V07-020 Sample catalogue prefers size_bytes (#133)

- Commits: 10db38fd
- Area: Data Loader (sample data catalogue)
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: The sample catalogue's Parquet conversion left stale CSV sizes in `size` beside the real counts in `size_bytes`, so every release rejected the catalogue (samples did not list).
- What changed: Data fixed upstream (ldaca-analytics-sample-data 4dc392f sets every `size` to the real byte count, keeps `size_bytes`, and `convert_to_parquet.py` writes both); submodule pointer bumped. `SampleFile` now uses `size_bytes` as `size` when it is present and an int, so a stale `size` cannot break the catalogue again.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A catalogue entry with `size` stale and `size_bytes` correct validates, and the file's size equals `size_bytes`.
  - The Data Loader lists the three sample collections.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/models/data_sources.py` `SampleFile.prefer_size_bytes` (pydantic `model_validator(mode="before")`); `ldaca-analytics-sample-data` submodule.
- Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_sample_data_integrity.py`.
- v0.8 notes: Alex's DuckDB views may rely on `size_bytes`; `size` is Wordflow's catalogue contract field. Keep both in step.

#### V07-021 LDaCA import: list all collections with access checks, metadata-only import (#135)

- Commits: 54bf549d, ad6148fd
- Area: Data Loader (LDaCA data portal import)
- Type: feature + behaviour change + bug fix
- Problem / reason: The LDaCA dialog asked users to pick a search mode and type a query although the portal holds few top-level collections. Restricted collections could not be imported at all (the collection-level RO-Crate lists no RepositoryObjects, error "RO-Crate contains no RepositoryObject metadata"). Found in app testing: ICE-AUS sections (W1A ... S2B) were flagged top-level but belong to ICE-AUS and have no RO-Crate (404); Australian Deafblind Signing Corpus publishes no item metadata ("lists no objects"); one ICE-AUS text (S1B-065.TXT) has a stray non-UTF-8 byte that failed the whole 1,069-document import.
- What changed:
  - The button/dialog is "Import LDaCA collections". It lists every top-level collection when it opens (new `POST /api/data-portal/collections`, body `{api_token?}`, returns `DataPortalSearchResource`). "Search by" and "Staff Picks" are removed. A "Filter collections" text box (placeholder "Name or description, e.g. COOEE") filters the list locally by name and description. Empty states: "No collections are available." / "No collections match the filter."
  - Only collections without a parent (`_memberOf`) are listed (17 instead of 29); importing ICE-AUS brings every section.
  - Records carry typed access for the current token: `has_access`, `access_group` (from Oni's `_access`), and requests carry `metadata_only`. Each row shows badges: "Restricted: <licence group label>" with a lock icon when the token cannot read it; the published item count ("N items" / "1 item"), or "Collection description only" when 0; and the licence.
  - Restricted rows show the text "Your API token cannot read this collection’s texts. Import its item metadata only, or update your token if you have been granted access." with buttons "Import metadata only" and "Update API token" (opens the credential panel inline; saving re-checks access). A restricted collection with no items shows "Your API token cannot read this collection, and it publishes no item metadata. Import the collection description, or update your token if you have been granted access." with "Import collection metadata". Accessible rows show "Download".
  - Metadata-only import: the worker lists member objects from the search index, fetches each member object's public RO-Crate with bounded concurrency, merges the graphs (entities deduplicated by `@id`), and flattens them with the existing RO-Crate tabulation (LDaCA Tabulator configs). `ldac:speaker` added to the general config's RepositoryObject `expand_props`, so speaker demographics become columns (Sydney Speaks: 261 rows x 52 columns, about 5 s, anonymous).
  - When a crate has no items, tabulation falls back to one row describing the collection, for full and metadata-only imports (Deafblind: 1 row x 32 columns).
  - Invalid UTF-8 bytes in imported texts become U+FFFD instead of failing the import.
  - Tutorial `data-loader.md` updated; two old paste-a-URL screenshots removed.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Opening the dialog lists 17 parentless collections without typing; typing "COOEE" filters locally.
  - With no/insufficient token, Sydney Speaks shows "Restricted" and "Import metadata only"; importing gives about 261 rows with speaker columns.
  - ICE-AUS full import gives 1,069 documents; ICE-AUS sections are not listed separately.
  - Deafblind shows "Collection description only" and "Import collection metadata", importing 1 row.
- Implementation: backend `api/data_portal.py` (`list_data_portal_collections`), `services/data_portal.py` (`collections`), `infrastructure/providers/oni.py` (listing, paging, access parsing, parent filter, item counts, object crate fetch), `workers/data_portal.py` (metadata-only merge, one-row fallback, U+FFFD decode), `workers/invocations.py`, `models/data_sources.py` (`DataPortalCollectionsRequest`, `has_access`, `access_group`, `metadata_only`, item count), `domain/user_file_import.py`, `resources/.../general/general-config.json`; frontend `data-loader/components/LdacaImportDialog.tsx`, `hooks/useLdacaImport.ts`, `hooks/ldacaImportState.ts`, `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`, `data-portal/components/DataPortalCredentialPanel.tsx`, `providerCredentialRequests.ts`, `tutorials/bundledRegistry.ts`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_oni_client.py` (listing and paging, access parsing, merging object crates, parentless filter, empty-crate fallback, non-UTF-8), `test_current_api_surface.py`; frontend `ldacaImportState.test.ts`, `useLdacaImport.test.tsx`, `DataLoaderFeature.test.tsx`, `providerCredentialRequests.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: The Oni portal specifics (`_access`, `_memberOf`, public member crates) are external facts to keep. Tabulation relies on the RO-Crate tabulator, independent of Polars/DuckDB.

#### V07-022 Folder and ZIP batch add: "Texts as one Data Block" or "Tables as separate Data Blocks" (#136)

- Commits: 26f31f8f, 738f77c6
- Area: Data Loader
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users wanted to add a folder (and, per a scope correction by Chao, a ZIP) of table files as separate Data Blocks, not only texts.
- What changed:
  - Adding a folder (the folder row "+" button) or a ZIP opens one dialog (`AddBatchPanel`), titled "Add Folder: <path>" or "Add ZIP: <path>", with tabs (aria-label "Add folder as" / "Add ZIP as"): "Texts as one Data Block" (default) and "Tables as separate Data Blocks (N)" (shows "…" while loading; disabled when there are no table files).
  - Texts description (folder): "Every .txt, .text, .md, .rst and .log file in this folder and its subfolders becomes one row of one Data Block. Other files, including ZIP archives, are skipped and listed after adding." (ZIP): "Every .txt, .text, .md, .rst and .log file in this ZIP becomes one row of one Data Block. Other files, including nested ZIP archives, are skipped and listed after adding." Confirm button "Add to Project".
  - Tables mode: description "Each selected table file becomes its own Data Block, named after the file. Select a file name to preview it."; lists every loadable table file recursively (CSV, TSV, JSON/JSONL, Parquet, Avro, Arrow/IPC, spreadsheets on their first sheet) with per-file checkboxes (aria-label "Add <file>"), "N of M selected", "Select all", "Select none", and a click-to-preview file name (aria-label "Preview <file>"). Confirm button "Add N Data Block(s)" (disabled at 0), "Adding…" while running. Each ticked file becomes its own Data Block named after its stem (relative path shown in the list). One summary toast "N Data Block(s) added to project." and, for failures, an error toast "N file(s) could not be added." with the failed paths as description.
  - A ZIP is never opened as a sub-folder: a folder skips (and in Texts mode reports) contained ZIPs; a ZIP skips nested ZIPs.
  - Folder Tables mode is frontend only (files come from the File Tree, each uses existing single-file creation). ZIP Tables mode adds backend: `GET /api/user-files/zip-tables?path=` returns `{members: [{path, size}]}` (same member safety checks as ZIP loading; hidden/OS members ignored; sorted); preview endpoint takes a `member` query; `FileNodeCreateRequest` gets `zip_member`. The member is extracted to a temporary file (named `member<ext>` so its name cannot escape) within the source/preview size limit ("ZIP member is too large for node ingestion"), then read by the standard loaders; the Data Block is named after the member stem.
  - Tutorial `data-loader.md` updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A folder with 2 CSVs and 1 XLSX in subfolders shows "Tables as separate Data Blocks (3)"; ticking two and confirming creates two Data Blocks named after the file stems and one success toast.
  - A folder with no table files has the Tables tab disabled.
  - A ZIP containing CSVs lists them under Tables mode; a nested ZIP is not listed.
  - Previewing a ZIP member shows its table.
- Implementation: frontend `data-loader/components/AddBatchPanel.tsx` (replaces `AddFolderPanel.tsx`), `AddFilePanel.tsx`, `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`, `hooks/useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.ts` (`handleAddFilesToWorkspace(paths, zipPath?)`), `hooks/useFilePreview.ts`, `hooks/useZipTableMembers.ts`, `utils/fileTreeHelpers.ts`, `common/hooks/useWorkspaceGraphMutations.ts` (`createNodeFromFile(path, sheet?, zipMember?)`), `lib/queryKeys.ts`; backend `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py` (`TABLE_EXTENSIONS`, `is_table_path`, `zip_table_members`, `extract_zip_table_member`), `api/files/preview.py` (`list_zip_table_members`, `member` param), `models/files.py` (`ZipTableMember`, `ZipTableMembersResource`), `models/node_resources.py` (`zip_member`), `services/file_preview.py`, `services/nodes.py` (`_load_zip_member_dataframe`).
- Tests: frontend `__tests__/AddBatchPanel.test.tsx` (both modes, Select all/none, previews), `useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.test.tsx` (summary toast), `utils/__tests__/fileTreeHelpers.test.ts`, `lib/__tests__/queryKeys.test.ts`; backend `tests/integration/test_folder_data_block.py` (ZIP tables list, preview, create), `test_current_api_surface.py`.
- v0.8 notes: Keep the two-mode dialog for both folders and ZIPs and the "never descend into ZIPs" rule. Loading uses the standard (Polars) file loaders per file; DuckDB `read_csv`/`read_parquet` etc. would be the analogue.

#### V07-023 File panel: move folders, multi-select, batch delete (#137, #138)

- Commits: 053371a0 (backend), 53f9d310 (frontend)
- Area: Data Loader (user file panel / File Tree)
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Folders could not be moved by drag and drop, and users had no way to act on many files at once (deleting was one request per file; there was a dedicated clear-root button).
- What changed:
  - Backend move accepts a real folder and moves it with its contents; moving a folder into itself or a subfolder is rejected ("A folder cannot be moved into itself"); a name clash at the destination stays a conflict (nothing merged or replaced). Folders are renamed under the per-user file gate; files keep hard-link moves.
  - New `POST /api/user-files/batch-delete` body `{paths: [1..10000]}` returns `{deleted: N}`: deletes many files and folders under one gate, covers entries inside a selected folder, skips ones that no longer exist, rejects the root ("The root folder cannot be deleted") and hidden paths.
  - Frontend: folder rows are draggable and move with their contents; empty panel space is a top-level (root) drop target. Drops that would put a folder inside itself are refused; internal drags stop at the row under the pointer so a rejected drop never falls through to the root (OS file drops still reach the upload zone).
  - Multi-select (Finder/Drive/VS Code style): row checkboxes (aria-label "Select <name>") shown on hover and on every row once anything is selected; Shift-click selects a range; Ctrl/Cmd-click toggles; "Select all at root" checkbox (sticky header row; label text "Select all at root" when nothing is selected) and per-folder "Select all in <folder>". The selection bar shows "N selected", a "Move to…" select (aria-label "Move selection to"; options "Root folder" plus valid folders), "Delete" and "Clear". Dragging a selected row moves the whole selection (selection roots); entries already in the target are skipped.
  - Keyboard: Esc clears the selection; Delete/Backspace opens the confirmation.
  - Delete asks once with a count: title "Delete <N files and M folders>?" (e.g. "Delete 312 files and 2 folders?"), description "Folders are deleted with everything inside them. This can’t be undone." (first sentence only when folders are selected), buttons "Cancel" / "Delete". Deletion is permanent (no trash or undo). "Select all at root" then Delete replaces the dedicated clear-root button.
  - Tutorial `data-loader.md` updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Dragging folder A onto folder B moves A and its contents into B; dragging A onto its own subfolder is refused.
  - Dropping on empty panel space moves to the root.
  - Shift-click selects a contiguous range; Cmd/Ctrl-click toggles one row; Esc clears.
  - Selecting all at root then Delete shows the counted confirmation and removes everything with one batch-delete request.
  - A name clash on move returns a conflict and changes nothing.
- Implementation: backend `api/files/crud.py` (`delete_files` route), `models/files.py` (`BatchDeleteFilesRequest`, `BatchDeleteFilesResource`), `services/user_files.py` (folder move, `delete_many`), `infrastructure/storage/safe_paths.py`; frontend `data-loader/components/FileTree.tsx` (selection state, `selectionRoots`, `canMoveInto`, `describeCount`, bulk delete dialog, props `onMoveMany`, `onDeleteMany`), `hooks/useFileBrowserActions.ts`, `utils/fileTreeHelpers.ts`, `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`.
- Tests: backend `test_user_file_store.py` (folder move, into-itself guard, conflict, batch delete), `test_current_api_surface.py`; frontend `components/__tests__/FileTreeSelection.test.tsx` (folder drops incl. into-itself guard, root drops, Select all at root with counted delete, Shift, Ctrl, Esc, multi-drag, per-folder select all), `FileTree.test.tsx`, `utils/__tests__/fileTreeHelpers.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: Keep server-side guards (into-itself, no merge on clash, root/hidden protection) and the single batch request. Verify drag/drop and Shift/Cmd selection in Safari/WebKit (Chao tests on Safari).

#### V07-024 Download a file selection as one ZIP that keeps its folders (#139)

- Commits: 9d221a68
- Area: Data Loader (user file panel), Export
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users could not download several files or folders at once.
- What changed:
  - The selection bar gains "Download". Selected files and folders become one ZIP whose paths start below the selection's common parent (e.g. top-level one.csv plus speeches/ give `one.csv` and `speeches/...`). ZIP filename: a single selected entry gives "<name>.zip" (a single folder 2020/ downloads as `2020.zip`); several entries give "<common parent name>.zip", or "wordflow_files.zip" when they share only the root. Links and hidden entries are left out; empty selected folders are kept. Deflate compression.
  - Two steps so desktop saves keep streaming natively through the existing GET-only command and long selections never go in a URL: `POST /api/user-files/archives` body `{paths}` validates the selection, checks size against the download limit ("Selection is too large to download as one ZIP", 413), and returns `{id, filename, file_count}` (201), a single-use id valid for 10 minutes (`_ARCHIVE_TTL_SECONDS = 600`); `GET /api/user-files/archives/{id}` builds the ZIP under the user's file gate into a response snapshot and streams it as `application/zip`, cleaning up afterwards.
  - Tutorial `data-loader.md` updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Selecting a folder `2020/` and clicking Download saves `2020.zip` containing `2020/...` paths relative to the common parent.
  - Reusing the archive id a second time, or after 10 minutes, fails (404).
  - Symlinks and hidden files in selected folders are not in the ZIP; empty folders are.
  - Works in the desktop app without Rust changes.
- Implementation: backend `api/files/crud.py` (`prepare_file_archive`, `download_file_archive`), `models/files.py` (`FileArchiveRequest`, `FileArchiveResource`), `services/user_files.py` (`prepare_archive`, `archive_snapshot`, `_common_parent`, `_archive_filename`, `_archive_members` using `os.walk(followlinks=False)`), `services/response_snapshots.py`; frontend `FileTree.tsx` (`onDownloadMany`), `hooks/useFileBrowserActions.ts`, `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`.
- Tests: backend `test_user_file_store.py`, `tests/integration/test_folder_data_block.py`, `test_current_api_surface.py`; frontend `FileTreeSelection.test.tsx`, `hooks/__tests__/useFileBrowserActions.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: Keep the register-then-GET pattern if the desktop shell only streams GET downloads natively.

#### V07-025 Open/Close projects, Import project, simpler panes, no ids in the graph (#140)

- Commits: 53153282
- Area: Data Loader (Project manager, Active Project pane), Project Graph, Docs
- Type: wording + behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Follow-on simplification after the Workspace to Project rename (#132): Load/Unload and Upload wording was unclear; the Active Project pane's Save only refreshed the project list (every change is already persisted); ids in the graph were noise.
- What changed:
  - Wording: "Load"/"Unload" become "Open"/"Close" (busy states "Opening…"/"Closing…"; reasons "Another project is being opened or closed.", "A task is still running on this project. Wait for it to finish, or cancel it from the task list, before closing it."; "Open a Project to access analysis Tabs.", "Open a project to use this view", "Open a project to add this file as a Data Block", "Open a project to add this folder as a Data Block"); "Failed to load:" becomes "Failed to open:"; toast `Project "<name>" was created, but could not be opened`; "Upload project" becomes "Import project" (busy "Importing…", aria-label "Import project archive"; toasts `Project "<file>" imported.`, "Project imported with <omissions> omitted.", "Failed to import project."). Hint text: "Opening restores the Project’s Data Blocks, Tabs, and analysis history. Choose Open beside the Project you want to continue."
  - The Active Project pane drops its "Save" and "Unload" buttons; its tooltip is now "Rename the open project or update its description. New data blocks are added here, and your work is saved automatically." Closing is done from the Project manager.
  - The Project manager always lists the open project first, then favourites, then most recently modified.

- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Project manager rows show "Open" (or "Close" for the open project); the open project is the first row.
  - The Active Project pane has no Save or Unload button.
  - Graph node cards show no id and no copy button.
  - The graph view title reads "Project Graph".

- Tests: `DataLoaderFeature.test.tsx`, `ActiveWorkspaceCard.test.tsx`, `FileTree.test.tsx`, `DesktopNavigationHeader.test.tsx` updated.
- v0.8 notes: Use Open/Close/Import vocabulary; no manual Save (autosave); no internal ids in the graph UI.

#### V07-069 Data Loader: spreadsheet blocks named after file and sheet (#181)

- Commits: 919cb08c
- Area: Data Loader
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: The backend named a file block `Path(file).stem` and ignored `sheet_name`, so two sheets of one workbook became two blocks with the same name (ANU tester feedback).
- What changed: A block from an Excel-family file (`.xlsx`, `.xls`, `.xlsm`, `.xlsb`, `.ods`, all detected as type `excel`) is named `<file stem>_<sheet>`. When no sheet is chosen, the first sheet's name is used (read with `fastexcel`). `/` and `\` in the sheet name become `-`. A name given by the user still wins. ZIP members are not renamed with a sheet. Files ticked inside a folder add their sheet name too (each is created as its own file request).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - `hansard.xlsx` with no sheet chosen and first sheet "Speeches" gives "hansard_Speeches".
  - With `sheet_name="Speakers_2020"` gives "hansard_Speakers_2020".
  - With `name="My block"` gives "My block".
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/services/nodes.py` (node creation in `NodeService`, new `_first_sheet_name(path)`), uses `detect_file_type` from `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py`. Docs: `tutorials/data-loader.md`.
- Tests: new `backend/tests/integration/test_excel_sheet_node_names.py::test_excel_blocks_are_named_after_file_and_sheet`.
- v0.8 notes: DuckDB's excel extension reads `.xlsx` only; keep the naming rule whatever reader is used. If the first sheet cannot be read the name falls back to the file stem.

#### V07-107 Data Loader: correct two misleading instructions (#205 item 10)

- Commits: c31f3432
- Area: Data Loader, Project manager
- Type: wording (misleading instruction fix)
- Problem / reason: the Tasks panel cannot stop analyses, yet the switch/close reason told users to "cancel it from the task list"; and the Create Project tooltip said a Project was needed before uploading, which is false.
- What changed (exact):

Wait for it to finish, or cancel it from the task list, before switching projects." to "An analysis is still running in this Project. Wait for it to finish, or stop it in its tab, before switching projects."; "A task is still running on this project. Wait for it to finish, or cancel it from the task list, before closing it." to "An analysis is still running in this Project. Wait for it to finish, or stop it in its tab, before closing the Project." (matches the Switch Project menu wording).
