Add an optional description if you want to capture its purpose." to "Create a Project to hold your Data Blocks and analyses. You can upload files before or after. Add an optional description to record its purpose."
- Expected behaviour (testable): with an analysis running, hovering the disabled switch/close control shows the new text; the create-project help tooltip shows the new text.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/features/views/data-loader/components/WorkspaceManagerItem.tsx`, `ActiveWorkspaceCard.tsx`.
- Tests: none.
- v0.8 notes: instructions must only name actions the UI actually offers; if v0.8 lets the Tasks panel stop analyses, the text can change accordingly.

#### V07-109 Data Loader: say why a file can't be read; quiet no-op type notes (#205 item 20)

- Commits: 31882bc4
- Area: Data Loader (add to Project, file preview), import type normalisation toast, shared Details dialog
- Type: bug fix / wording
- Problem / reason: "User file could not be loaded" and "File preview could not be generated" hid the real reason (not UTF-8, encrypted ZIP, bad CSV and so on). The import toast said "N columns were normalized to standard dtypes" and listed "Int64", "Float32 widened to Float64", "naive datetime assumed UTC".
- What changed:
  - Backend `describe_load_failure(exc, filename)` (`infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py`) walks the cause chain and returns, in order of checks:
    - UnicodeError or "utf-8"/"utf8": "The file isn't saved as UTF-8 text. Save it again as UTF-8 (in Excel: CSV UTF-8) and upload it again."
    - "encrypted"/"password": "The ZIP file is password-protected. Unzip it, or save it again without a password, then upload it again."
    - `zipfile.BadZipFile`: "The ZIP file is damaged or incomplete. Create it again and upload it."
    - `pl.exceptions.NoDataError` or "empty": "The file is empty."
    - Excel file or `fastexcel.FastExcelError`: "Wordflow couldn't read this spreadsheet. Open it in Excel, save it again as .xlsx, and upload it again."
    - json/jsonl or "json": "The JSON file isn't valid. Check it with a JSON validator, then upload it again."
    - other `PolarsError`: "Wordflow couldn't read the rows of this file. Check that every row has the same number of columns and that text containing commas is in quotes."
    - fallback: "Wordflow couldn't read this file. Check that it isn't damaged, then try again."
  - Add to Project (`services/nodes.py`): "Couldn't load {filename}. {reason}" with `details.diagnostic`. Preview (`services/file_preview.py`): "Couldn't preview {filename}. {reason}" with `details.diagnostic`. `RuntimeError` (zipfile's password-protected member error) is now in the caught load errors, so it is a 400 with that reason instead of a 500.
  - Normalisation reasons reworded: "naive datetime assumed UTC" to "the times have no time zone, so Wordflow reads them as UTC"; "converted from {tz} to UTC" to "the times were converted from {tz} to UTC"; time precision to "stored to the microsecond (a change you won't see)"; integer promotion to "{kind} whole numbers stored in the standard size, so Data Blocks from different files line up (a change you won't see)"; Float32 widening to "decimals stored in the standard size, so Data Blocks from different files line up (a change you won't see)".
  - Frontend `notifyTypeAdjustments(changes)`: only time-zone changes are announced (reason contains "time zone" or "converted from"). Toast (info, 10 s): "We adjusted 1 column type so Wordflow can use it." / "We adjusted {n} column types so Wordflow can use them.", with a Details link opening a dialog titled "Column types adjusted", explanation "How Wordflow stores these columns. The Data Editor shows each column's type.", lines "{column}: {reason}.", no Send feedback.
  - Shared Details dialog generalised: store holds `{ text, title, explanation, feedback }`; `DetailsDialogButton` exported; errors use title "Error details" with Send feedback.
  - Data Loader notify calls pass the error object as `cause`, so the backend message and Details show (fallbacks like "Failed to add file to project." remain as fallbacks; later reworded by item 25). File preview errors render with `ErrorNotice` (fallback "Couldn't load the preview.").
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Previewing a Latin-1 CSV shows "Couldn't preview x.csv. The file isn't saved as UTF-8 text. …" with Details.
  - Adding a CSV with ragged rows shows "Couldn't load x.csv. Wordflow couldn't read the rows of this file. …".
  - A password-protected ZIP member returns HTTP 400 with the password message.
  - Importing a file with only Int32 columns shows no type toast; a file with naive datetimes shows "We adjusted 1 column type so Wordflow can use it." with Details.
- Implementation: backend `infrastructure/storage/data_loading.py`, `services/file_preview.py`, `services/nodes.py`; frontend `components/errors/ErrorDetails.tsx`, `stores/errorDetailsStore.ts`, `features/workspace/common/notifyTypeAdjustments.tsx` (new), `useWorkspaceGraphMutations.ts`, Data Loader hooks (`useDataLoaderWorkspaceActions.ts`, `useFileBrowserActions.ts`, `useFilePreview.ts`, `useFolderCreation.ts`, `useLdacaImport.ts`, `useUploadState.ts`), `DataLoaderFeature.tsx`, `FilePreviewContent.tsx`.
- Tests: backend `test_canonical_node_api.py`, `test_error_diagnostics.py`, `test_files_preview.py`; frontend `features/workspace/common/__tests__/notifyTypeAdjustments.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: the reason detection matches Polars/fastexcel/zipfile exceptions and message text; DuckDB's CSV/JSON readers raise different errors (for example `InvalidInputException`, "Could not convert string"), so the mapping must be rebuilt for DuckDB while keeping the same user sentences. Type normalisation in v0.8 will differ; still only announce changes users would notice.

#### V07-153 Remove the 64 MiB file size limit for adding and previewing files (#236)

- Commits: 54eb3dfd
- Area: Data Loader and files
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: A single file over 64 MiB could not be previewed or added as a Data Block (`resource_too_large`, HTTP 413), for example a 500 MB Parquet file. The limit was the `max_preview_source_bytes` setting from the v0.7 backend refactor (16 July 2026, first shipped in v0.7.0), shared by preview and ingestion.
- What changed:
  - `FileReadService` and `NodeService` no longer check a single file's size. `FileReadService` takes `max_expanded_bytes` instead of `max_preview_bytes`; `NodeService` drops `max_source_bytes`.
  - Folders of texts and ZIP members still have a bound, because they expand into new bytes (ZIP bombs): the Data Block storage limit, `max_node_storage_bytes` (1 GiB).
  - The `max_preview_source_bytes` setting is removed (`docs/reference/backend-settings.md` updated).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A Parquet file over 64 MiB previews and becomes a Data Block. The real 500 MB, 6.9 million row Hansard `paragraph.parquet` was added in under a second, with a peak of about 4.4 GB extra memory for the eager load.
  - A folder of texts larger than the Data Block storage limit fails with "The folder is too large to add as a Data Block".
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/services/file_preview.py`, `services/nodes.py`, `runtime.py`, `settings.py`.
- Tests: `tests/integration/test_file_size_limits.py` (both cases fail on the old code).
- v0.8 notes: do not reintroduce a per-file source size limit. Stream file ingestion (scan, normalise lazily, sink) so memory stays small whatever the file size; the eager load is the real constraint on small machines.

### Data Editor

#### V07-026 Delete several columns at once (#141)

- Commits: 82831184
- Area: Data Editor (then still called Data view)
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users could only delete one column at a time from the column menu; each deletion was a separate Undo step.
- What changed: A "Delete columns" button (icon Columns3, title "Delete several columns at once") sits before Undo in the Data view header. It opens a dialog titled "Delete columns" with description "Choose the columns to remove from this Data Block. Undo restores them in one step.", a "Filter columns" input, a "{n} of {total} selected" count, "Select all" (adds the currently visible, filtered columns) and "Select none" buttons, a scrolling checkbox list ("No columns match the filter." when empty), and a red confirm button "Delete {n} column(s)". If every column is ticked, an alert "Keep at least one column." shows and the confirm button is disabled. The button is disabled when the block has fewer than 2 columns. Backend adds a `delete_columns` edit so the batch is one Data Block Edit and one Undo.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Ticking 3 columns and confirming removes all 3; one Undo restores all 3.
  - Ticking all columns disables the confirm button and shows "Keep at least one column."; the backend also refuses with "Keep at least one column on the Data Block".
  - Unknown column names are rejected with "Columns to delete are not present on the Data Block: a, b"; duplicate names in the request are rejected ("Columns to delete must be unique").
  - Select all with a filter active only ticks the filtered columns.
  - Closing the dialog clears the selection and filter.
- Implementation: backend `DeleteColumnsNodeEditRequest` (kind `delete_columns`, `columns` 1..10000 unique) in `models/node_resources.py`; `build_edited_lazyframe` in `services/node_operations.py` does `node.data.drop(columns)`. Frontend `DeleteColumnsDialog.tsx`, `WorkspaceDataHeader.tsx` (`onDeleteColumns`), `useWorkspaceTransformMutations.deleteColumns`, `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts`. Tutorial `ui.md`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/integration/test_delete_columns_edit.py::test_delete_columns_is_one_edit_and_keeps_a_column`; `frontend/.../data-view/components/__tests__/DeleteColumnsDialog.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: Keep "one batch = one undo step" and the "at least one column remains" rule (a block with zero columns cannot keep its rows). Pure column drop; DuckDB equivalent is a projection without those columns.

#### V07-027 Data Editor: in-place column tools move out of Preprocessing, with live edit preview (#143)

- Commits: d13d63ab (backend), 7ca8d6d3 (frontend)
- Area: Data Editor, Data Builder (Preprocessing), Docs
- Type: feature / behaviour change
- Problem / reason: After #129, Preprocessing tools always created new blocks, while its Find and Create sub-tabs always edited the selected block in place. The in-place single-block column operations belong with other per-block edits (rename, cast, delete columns) in the table itself, as in spreadsheets and OpenRefine. Feedback also said "Data view" suggests inspection only.
- What changed:
  - The Data view header now reads "Data Editor" (help label "Data Editor"). Toolbar: "Add column" dropdown ("Combine columns…", "Duplicate column…", "Extract text…", "Split column…"), "Find & replace" button, "Clean text" dropdown (one item per cleaning operation), "Delete columns", "Undo", "Redo". The header toolbar is `flex-wrap`.
  - Each column header's settings menu gains "Find & replace…", "Clean text…", "Extract text…", "Split…", "Duplicate…" (column pre-filled) above Rename/Delete.
  - A tool opens a panel in place of the Project Graph (top-right). Header: tool label " · <block name>", a "Show Project Graph" button, and a close button ("Close tool"). While the graph is shown, a bar "Back to <tool label>" returns to the tool. The panel is kept mounted (hidden) while the graph is shown.
  - While the form is valid the table below shows a server preview of the edited page; added or changed columns are tinted (header `bg-button/20`, cells `bg-button/10`, `data-preview-column` attribute). Footer status: "Complete the settings to preview the result below." / "Previewing…" / "N row(s) changed" (whole block). "Apply" commits one Data Block Edit (toast "<tool> applied to <block>."); "Cancel" discards.
  - Switching to another Data Block: if the tool is untouched it closes silently; if touched, an alert "Discard your unfinished <tool> on <block>?" with description "The settings have not been applied. Keep editing to return to that Data Block." and buttons "Keep editing" (re-selects and activates the tool's block) / "Discard". Drafts are never kept per block.
  - Tool labels: "Find & replace", "Extract text", "Combine columns", "Duplicate column", "Clean text", "Split column".
  - Duplicate: no options; copy named Finder-style "<col> copy", "<col> copy 2", ... and inserted right of the original. Panel text: 'The copy is placed right of the original and named like a copied file, for example "<col> copy".'
  - Clean text operations (labels): "Trim spaces at the start and end", "Collapse repeated spaces", "lowercase", "UPPERCASE", "Title Case", "Remove punctuation and symbols", "Remove digits", "Remove web links (URLs)", "Remove HTML tags". Output: "Save result to" radio "The same column" / "A new column, right of it" (+ name box).
  - Split column (first version): "Split on" text, "Number of columns" 2..50; outputs `<col>_1..<col>_n` right of the source, last keeps the remainder. (Reworked in #146 and #163.)
  - Find & replace / Extract text (first version, regex only): labels "Find (regular expression)", "Replace with", "Only the first match in each row"; Extract: "Extract matches of (regular expression)", "Join several matches with" (default " "), "New column name". Find's Extract output now sits right of its source.
  - Combine columns (first version: ordered checkbox list + "Separator"), replaced the same day by the template (next entry).
  - Preprocessing keeps only Filter, Sample, Join and Stack; the Find and Create sub-tabs, their contextual hints ("Transform text with a pattern", "Build an analysis-ready column", "Your Data Block was updated") and help entries are removed; `ui.md` and `preprocessing.md` point to the Data Editor.
  - New endpoint `POST /workspaces/{ws}/nodes/{id}/edits/preview?page&page_size` (page_size 1..500, default 50) runs any `NodeEditRequest` without applying it, returns an Arrow page plus header `X-Wordflow-Changed-Rows` (exposed via CORS).
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Duplicating "text" twice gives "text copy" then "text copy 2", each directly right of "text".
  - Clean text into a new column places it right of the source; into the same column replaces values in place; an existing output name is refused ("Output column name already exists on the Data Block").
  - Split refuses if any `<col>_i` already exists ("Split would overwrite existing columns: ...").
  - The preview never changes the stored block; Undo after Apply restores the previous plan.
  - "N rows changed" counts rows where an edited column (a column named in the request) differs (null-aware) or a new column is non-null and non-empty, over the whole block, not just the page.
  - Selecting another block with a touched tool shows the Keep editing / Discard prompt; with an untouched tool, the tool just closes.
  - Preprocessing shows only Filter, Sample, Join, Stack tabs.
- Implementation: backend request models `DuplicateColumnNodeEditRequest`, `CleanTextNodeEditRequest` (`CleanTextOperation` literal), `SplitColumnNodeEditRequest` in `models/node_resources.py`; `build_edited_lazyframe`, `duplicate_column_name`, `_place_right_of` (pure `select` reorder), `_clean_text_expression` in `services/node_operations.py`; `NodeService.preview_edit`, `_preview_edit`, `_count_changed_rows` (casts compared columns to String, `ne_missing`, `any_horizontal(...).sum()`) in `services/nodes.py`; route `preview_node_edit` in `api/workspaces/nodes.py`; `CHANGED_ROWS_HEADER` in `shared/table_transport.py`. Frontend: zustand store `dataEditorToolStore.ts` (tool, nodeId, request, highlightColumns, changedRows, dirty, graphVisible), pure builders `dataEditorRequests.ts` (`buildFindReplace`, `buildExtract`, `buildDuplicate`, `buildCleanText`, `buildSplit`, `duplicateColumnName`), `DataEditorToolPanel.tsx`, `WorkspaceView.tsx` (swaps the graph for the panel), `WorkspaceDataHeader.tsx`, `WorkspaceColumnHeader.tsx` (`COLUMN_TOOLS`), `WorkspaceTable.tsx` (highlight), `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts` (preview query with `placeholderData` keep-previous, switch guard), `api/tableApi.ts::previewNodeEditTable`. Removed `preprocessing/aggregate/*` and `preprocessing/replace/*`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/integration/test_data_editor_edits.py` (`test_preview_reports_changed_rows_without_applying`, `test_duplicate_split_and_clean_apply_as_single_edits`), `backend/tests/unit/test_current_api_surface.py`; frontend `data-view/__tests__/dataEditorRequests.test.ts`, `data-view/components/__tests__/DataEditorToolPanel.test.tsx`, updated `guidance/__tests__/registry.test.tsx`, `DataPreprocessingFeature.test.tsx`, `WorkspaceDataHeader.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: The UX contract to preserve: one tool open at a time, preview in the real table with highlighted columns, whole-block changed-row count, Apply = one undoable edit, discard prompt on block switch, new columns inserted right of their source. The changed-row count is a Polars lazy comparison; in DuckDB it is a single `SELECT count(*) WHERE a IS DISTINCT FROM b OR ...` over the edited projection. Punctuation removal uses Rust-regex Unicode classes `[\p{P}\p{S}]`; DuckDB's RE2 supports `\pP`/`\pS` but check syntax. Title Case in v0.7 is a workaround (see Title Case entry) that is not needed in DuckDB.

#### V07-028 Combine columns uses a template with column suggestions (#143 follow-up)

- Commits: 8dd3d9f3
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: feature / behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Feedback on the first Combine: an ordered checkbox list with one separator could not put different text between columns, and long column lists needed scrolling.
- What changed: Combine columns is a "Template" textarea such as `{title}: {body}` (placeholder "e.g. {title}: {body}", monospace). Typing "{" opens a "Matching columns" listbox (prefix matches first, then substring matches, max 8); ArrowUp/Down move, Enter or Tab inserts, Escape dismisses; a closing "}" already present is skipped. A searchable "Insert column" picker (search placeholder "Find a column… (* and ? wildcards)") inserts at the caret. `{{` and `}}` are literal braces. Help line: "Type { to pick a column, or use Insert column. Other text is kept as written." / "Uses a, b. Write {{ or }} for a literal brace." Errors: "Write }} for a literal closing brace.", "A { has no matching }.", "Put a column name inside the braces.", "Not a column on this Data Block: x". Plus "New column name" and "When a value is missing": "Treat it as blank text" (default) / "Leave the combined value empty". The panel shows "First row on this page: "<value>"" (or italic "empty"). Every Data Editor tool's column picker becomes a searchable select. When opened from a column menu the template starts as `{<col>}`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - `{title}: {body}` on a row title="A", body="B" gives "A: B".
  - Columns of any type (numbers, dates) join as text.
  - With "Treat it as blank text", a null column contributes ""; with "Leave the combined value empty", any null part makes the result null.
  - A template with no column reference, an unknown column, or an unbalanced brace does not preview and Apply is disabled.
  - The new column name must be new; backend refuses existing names ("New column name already exists on the Data Block").
  - The combined column is appended at the end of the block (not right of a source).
- Implementation: backend `CombineTextPart`, `CombineColumnPart`, `CombineColumnsNodeEditRequest` (kind `combine_columns`, `parts` 1..200, `output_column`, `empty_values` "blank" | "empty_result") in `models/node_resources.py`; `pl.concat_str` of `pl.col(c).cast(String)` and `pl.lit(text)` with optional `fill_null("")` in `node_operations.py`. Frontend `CombineTemplateField.tsx` (combobox ARIA pattern), `parseCombineTemplate`, `templateColumnToken`, `buildCombine` in `dataEditorRequests.ts`; `previewSample` in `dataEditorToolStore.ts` and `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_edits.py::test_combine_columns_template_handles_types_and_missing_values`; `dataEditorRequests.test.ts`, `DataEditorToolPanel.test.tsx`.
- v0.8 notes: `concat_str` without `ignore_nulls` returns null if any part is null; DuckDB `concat` skips nulls while `||` propagates them, so pick the operator to match each "missing value" option. Keep the template grammar (braces, doubled braces as literals) so saved user habits carry over.

#### V07-029 Title Case edits survive Project save and load (#143 follow-up)

- Commits: 99f475d7
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: `str.to_titlecase` saved fine but polars-source-utils could not read the plan back ("unknown variant Titlecase"), so reopening the Project failed.
- What changed: Title Case is rebuilt from supported ops: mark each word (`\w+(?:['’]\w+)*`) with a separator, split, uppercase the first character and lowercase the rest of each piece, join. Apostrophes inside words are kept ("It's" not "It'S").
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - "it's a TEST" becomes "It's A Test".
  - A Project containing a Title Case edit reopens without error.
  - Every Data Editor edit kind serializes and reads back through polars-source-utils.
- Implementation: `_title_case` in `services/node_operations.py` (uses `_SPLIT_MARK = "\x1f"` and `list.eval`).
- Tests: new `backend/tests/unit/test_data_editor_saved_plans.py` (`test_edit_plan_can_be_read_back` parametrised over every edit, `test_title_case_keeps_apostrophes_inside_words`).
- v0.8 notes: Polars/polars-source-utils specific workaround; DuckDB can implement Title Case directly (e.g. regex or list functions) but keep the apostrophe rule. The "every plan must round-trip" regression test is the important idea to carry over for whatever v0.8 persists.

#### V07-031 Find & replace and Extract text match plain text by default (#145)

- Commits: 980f492d (part)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: Many users do not know regular expressions and mistakes are silent: typing "." to remove the dot before a file extension matched every character and emptied the column.
- What changed: Labels become "Find" and "Extract matches of" with a "Use regular expression" checkbox (unchecked by default). Placeholders: plain "e.g. .txt" / "e.g. #auspol"; regex "e.g. \s+" / "e.g. #\w+". In plain mode the pattern is literal and "$" in the replacement is literal (no `$1` group references). Requests carry `literal: true`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Find "." replace "" on "a.txt" gives "atxt" in plain mode; with regex ticked it gives "".
  - Replacement "$1" in plain mode inserts the text "$1".
  - Extract "#auspol" in plain mode extracts only that literal text.
  - Older saved plans without the flag keep regex behaviour.
- Implementation: `ReplaceDerivation.literal: bool = False` in `domain/workspace/provenance.py`; `_replace_expression` uses `str.replace_all(..., literal=)`, `str.replace(..., literal=)`, and `pl.escape_regex` for extract. Frontend `RegexOption` in `DataEditorToolPanel.tsx`; `buildFindReplace`/`buildExtract` set `literal: !regex`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_edits.py::test_count_and_plain_text_replace`; `dataEditorRequests.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: Default must stay plain text. Backward compatibility: absent flag means regex. Polars regex is Rust `regex`; DuckDB uses RE2 (no backreferences in patterns, replacement group syntax `\1` rather than `$1`), so regex-mode semantics may differ slightly.

#### V07-032 Split column on several delimiters, from the left or the right (#146)

- Commits: 980f492d (part)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Extend Split column like spreadsheet Text to Columns and Python split/rsplit.
- What changed: Request has `delimiters` (1..20 unique plain-text strings) and `direction` "left" | "right". UI at this commit: "Split on any of" checkboxes Comma (default ticked), Semicolon, Space, Tab, "Pipe ( | )", New line, plus "Other text" (placeholder "e.g. :: or  - "); "Split from" radios "The left (the last column keeps the rest)" / "The right (the first column keeps the rest)". Help: "Text left over after the split keeps its original delimiters." (Checkboxes replaced by chips in #163.)
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - "a,b;c d" with delimiters [",", ";"] into 2 columns from the left gives "a" and "b;c d".
  - From the right into 2 columns gives "a,b" and "c d" (remainder keeps delimiters); a short row fills from the left as Python rsplit does.
  - When two delimiters match at the same position, the longest wins (", " over ",").
  - Leading or trailing delimiters produce empty parts; there is no "treat consecutive delimiters as one" option.
  - Missing parts are null.
- Implementation: `_delimiter_pattern` (escaped alternation, longest first), `_split_pieces` (insert `\x1f` after each delimiter, split), `_split_parts` (list.get / slice / join, strips trailing delimiter per part) in `node_operations.py`; `SplitColumnNodeEditRequest` in `node_resources.py`; `buildSplit` in `dataEditorRequests.ts`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_edits.py::test_split_on_several_delimiters_from_either_side`; saved-plan round trip for both directions.
- v0.8 notes: The marker-character trick is a Polars workaround to keep which delimiter was used; in DuckDB, `regexp_split_to_array` loses delimiters, so a different approach is needed to keep the remainder's original delimiters. Preserve the rsplit fill rule.

#### V07-033 Count tool: words, characters, or matches (#147)

- Commits: 980f492d (part)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users wanted a count column without writing expressions.
- What changed: New tool "Count" in Add column ("Count words, characters, or matches…") and in each column menu ("Count…"). "Count" radios: "Words", "Characters", "Characters, not counting spaces", "Matches of a text or pattern" (then "Text to count" with placeholder "e.g. !" or regex "e.g. \bthe\b", and "Use regular expression"). Note: "Words are runs of text between spaces or line breaks, as word processors count them." "New column name" defaults (placeholder, used when left blank) to "<col> word count", "<col> character count", "<col> character count (no spaces)", "<col> match count". Output placed right of the source.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - "Hello  big world" gives Words 3, Characters 16, Characters not counting spaces 13.
  - Matches of "!" (plain) on "Hi!!" gives 2.
  - A null text gives a null count, not 0.
  - Leaving the name blank creates "text word count" for column "text".
- Implementation: `CountNodeEditRequest` (kind `count`, measure, pattern, regex, output_column) in `node_resources.py`; `_count_expression` (`count_matches(r"\S+")`, `len_chars`, whitespace removal, `count_matches(literal=not regex)`) in `node_operations.py`; `COUNT_MEASURES`, `defaultCountName`, `buildCount` in `dataEditorRequests.ts`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_edits.py::test_count_and_plain_text_replace`; saved-plan test covers count.
- v0.8 notes: Keep null-in, null-out. Character counts are Unicode characters (`len_chars`), not bytes; DuckDB `length()` counts characters, `strlen()` bytes.

#### V07-042 Data Editor: scroll the preview column into view; keep focus in the tool panel (#154)

- Commits: d8ff5f1e, af7ffed8 (scroll part), 69c5be2c, da5fa715
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: behaviour change / bug fix (Safari)
- Problem / reason: On a wide block the highlighted preview column could be off-screen. Follow-ups: opening a tool from a menu handed focus back to the menu trigger after the first keystroke, so typing a new column name stopped after one letter; after picking a "{" suggestion, a key typed immediately landed before it ("{a} + " became "{a}+ "); the Chromium-only fixes did not hold in Safari.
- What changed (final state):
  - While a tool previews, the table scrolls so the source column (`scrollAnchor`) sits at the left edge of the table (after left-pinned columns), so the changed or new column beside it shows. Combine columns (no source) scrolls to the far right end, where it adds its column. Every new preview (any settings change, keyed by the JSON request) realigns; paging through a preview keeps the position.
  - Safari workaround: for up to 1500 ms after aligning, the alignment is reapplied when the table or viewport resizes (ResizeObserver) or the scroll position drifts by more than 1px, until the user wheels, presses a pointer, or presses a key inside the scroll area.
  - Tool menus (header dropdowns and column menus) cancel Radix's `onCloseAutoFocus` and call `focusDataEditorTool()`, which focuses the first field of `[data-editor-tool-form]` (textarea, text input, or combobox button, excluding "Insert column"); the panel also calls it on mount. If focus is already inside the panel (Safari runs the menu close callback late), it does nothing.
  - Combine template: caret after an autocomplete insertion is set in `useLayoutEffect` in the same commit (no timer); the Insert column picker path keeps the delayed focus because focus is in its popup.
  - History: d8ff5f1e first aligned the last highlighted column to the right edge only when the highlighted set changed; af7ffed8 made it realign on every preview; da5fa715 switched to source-at-left-edge (as Chao proposed) and added the Safari settling window.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Clean text on "text" in a wide block: "text" is at the left edge of the viewport after the preview arrives.
  - Duplicate: source at the left edge, copy visible beside it.
  - Combine columns: table scrolled to the end, new column visible.
  - Scroll away, change an option: the alignment comes back; change page: it does not.
  - Open a tool from a menu and type a name quickly: every character lands in the field (verified in WebKit and Chromium).
  - Picking "{user_id}" from suggestions then typing " + " gives "{user_id} + ".
- Implementation: `WorkspaceTable.tsx` (`previewScroll` prop `{key, column}`, `scrolledKeyRef`, `stopSettlingRef`, ResizeObserver, listeners on `[data-slot="scroll-area"]`), `dataEditorRequests.ts` (`scrollAnchor` per builder: source column, `null` for combine), `dataEditorToolStore.ts` (`scrollAnchor`), `useWorkspaceDataTable.ts`, new `focusDataEditorTool.ts`, `WorkspaceDataHeader.tsx` (`keepToolFocus`), `WorkspaceColumnHeader.tsx` (`openedToolRef`), `CombineTemplateField.tsx` (`pendingCaretRef`), `DataEditorToolPanel.tsx` (`data-editor-tool-form`).
- Tests: `data-view/components/__tests__/WorkspaceTable.test.tsx` (scroll cases), `DataEditorToolPanel.test.tsx`, `dataEditorRequests.test.ts`; manual Playwright WebKit and Chromium runs.
- v0.8 notes: WebKit-specific: Safari moves scrollLeft back while re-rendering rows and settles column widths late, and runs Radix menu close callbacks late. Any v0.8 table that auto-scrolls must be verified in Playwright WebKit (Chao uses Safari). Keep "user interaction cancels auto-alignment".

#### V07-043 Clean text: Remove XML tags and markup (#155)

- Commits: af7ffed8 (part)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Users with XML corpora need to strip markup and keep text; "Remove HTML tags" (`<[^>]+>`) breaks on quoted attributes containing ">" and leaves entities.
- What changed: New Clean text option "Remove XML tags and markup" (`remove_xml_tags`). Removes element tags (namespaced names, quoted attributes that may contain ">", self-closing), XML declarations and processing instructions (`<?...?>`), comments, DOCTYPE with or without an internal subset, and CDATA wrappers (keeping inner text); then decodes `&lt; &gt; &quot; &apos; &amp;` (with "&amp;" last so "&amp;lt;" becomes "&lt;"). Sequential Analysis info page now points to Clean text instead of a regex.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - `<?xml version="1.0"?><!-- c --><a:b x="1>2">Tom &amp; Jerry</a:b><![CDATA[x<y]]>` becomes `Tom & Jerry x<y` (no separator inserted; exact spacing follows the input).
  - The plan survives Project save and load.
- Implementation: `_XML_MARKUP_PATTERN`, `_XML_ENTITIES`, branch in `_clean_text_expression` in `node_operations.py`; `CLEAN_TEXT_OPERATIONS` in `dataEditorRequests.ts`.
- Tests: `test_data_editor_saved_plans.py::test_remove_xml_tags_keeps_text_and_decodes_entities` and round trip.
- v0.8 notes: The pattern uses `[\s\S]*?` lazy quantifiers, supported by both Rust regex and RE2. Numeric character references (`&#123;`) are not decoded.

#### V07-049 Split column: typed delimiter chips; Number of columns beside Column (#163)

- Commits: bb61d219
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: behaviour change
- Problem / reason: "Number of columns" sat below Split from and scrolled out of the panel; the preset checkboxes plus "Other text" were clumsy.
- What changed: "Delimiters" chip input (default chip ","): type punctuation, a space, or several characters and press Enter to add; duplicates ignored; "x" ("Remove delimiter <label>") or Backspace in the empty field removes the last chip. Placeholder "Type a delimiter, then Enter" / "Add another"; help "Press Enter after each delimiter: punctuation, a space, or several characters." A lone space shows as "space", a lone tab as "tab", and spaces/tabs inside longer delimiters as "␣"/"⇥" (". " shows as ".␣"). One predefined checkbox "Also split at each new line". "Number of columns" (2..50) sits beside "Column"; "Split from" radios on one line.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Typing ";" Enter adds a ";" chip; typing ";" Enter again adds nothing.
  - Backspace in the empty input removes the last chip.
  - Ticking "Also split at each new line" adds "\n" to the request delimiters.
- Implementation: new `DelimiterChips.tsx`; `DataEditorToolPanel.tsx`; `SPLIT_DELIMITER_PRESETS` removed from `dataEditorRequests.ts`.
- Tests: `DataEditorToolPanel.test.tsx` (chip cases).
- v0.8 notes: Frontend only; backend request shape from #146 unchanged.

#### V07-050 Data Editor: new-column options preview under a default name (#164)

- Commits: 27663b1c (7c6879b2 fixed the Tab race it exposed; see #156 entry)
- Area: Data Editor
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: Choosing "A new column, right of it" in Find & replace showed no preview ("Complete the settings…") because the name started empty, and the name box sat below the option, out of view on a short panel.
- What changed: New-column names default (placeholder, used when blank) to "<col> replaced" (Find & replace), "<col> cleaned" (Clean text), "<col> matches" (Extract text), and preview straight away; Tab accepts the default for editing. The name box shares the option's line.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Find "_" in "username" with A new column previews a "username replaced" column right of "username"; typing a name renames it.
  - Extract text previews immediately under "<col> matches" once a pattern is entered.
- Implementation: `defaultNewColumnName` in `dataEditorRequests.ts`; `OutputChoice` and builders in `DataEditorToolPanel.tsx`.
- Tests: `DataEditorToolPanel.test.tsx` (new-column default cases).

#### V07-051 Dates: show Date columns as dates; convert them to datetime directly (#165)

- Commits: 86246a5d
- Area: Data Editor (table display, type conversion)
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: User feedback (Excel metadata with a DD/MM/YYYY column). Arrow Date values reached the table as epoch milliseconds (31 Jan 2020 showed as 1580428800000). Converting a Date column to datetime failed ("Sample validation failed ... expected String, got date") because the cast always used `str.strptime`.
- What changed: Arrow Date values render as "YYYY-MM-DD". Date to Datetime casts directly (`cast(pl.Datetime("us"))`); Date to text with a format uses strftime like datetimes. The plan reads back for Project save and load.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A Date cell for 2020-01-31 displays "2020-01-31".
  - Converting a Date column to datetime succeeds and yields midnight datetimes.
  - Converting a Date column to text with "%d/%m/%Y" gives "31/01/2020".
- Implementation: `normalizeArrowValue` in `frontend/src/lib/arrow/arrowTable.ts` (`DataType.isDate` then `new Date(ms).toISOString().slice(0, 10)`); `_datetime_cast_expr` and `_cast_expr` in `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/services/node_casting.py`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_node_casting.py::test_date_columns_convert_to_datetime_without_text_parsing`; `frontend/src/lib/arrow/__tests__/arrowTable.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: The frontend conversion uses UTC, which is correct for epoch-day values; do not use local time. DuckDB DATE to TIMESTAMP is a direct cast.

#### V07-062 Missing values shown as empty, not "null" (#176)

- Commits: a7fc7002
- Area: Tables, Data Builder (Filter, previews), Data Editor, Row Details, Docs
- Type: wording / behaviour change
- Problem / reason: "Null" is computer-science jargon for HASS researchers. #166 had already made Filter treat missing, NaN and blank text as one "empty" idea, but other places still said `(null)`, `(undefined)`, `(empty string)`, `NaN` or `null`.
- What changed:
  - Preview tables (`formatPreviewValue`, used by Filter, Join, Stack and other Data Builder previews) show missing (null/undefined), NaN and blank/whitespace-only text as an empty cell.
  - Row Details metadata shows missing and NaN as empty (was `null`).
  - Data Editor "First row on this page:" sample shows italic "empty" for null, NaN and blank text (NaN used to show as "null" via `JSON.stringify`).
  - Filter value checklist: the missing-value entry is "(empty)" (was "Null (no value)"); a blank or whitespace-only text value is "(blank text)" (was "(empty string)").
  - Filter missing-values toast description: "Rows with missing values won’t match ordinary filter conditions. Choose “is empty” to target them." (was "is null").
  - Auto-generated block names use `empty` for a null condition value (was `null`).
  - Concordance tutorial: "Sorting is case-sensitive, and empty values come first in either direction." (replaces "Polars' default null ordering").
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - A preview cell holding null, undefined, NaN, "" or "   " renders as an empty string.
  - `formatPreviewValue(0)` is "0", `false` is "false", `{a:1}` is `{"a":1}`.
  - Filter checklist for values `['', '  ', 'a']` with nulls present lists "(empty)", "(blank text)", "(blank text)", "a" (the two blank strings remain separate entries).
  - The Data Editor sample says "empty" for NaN.
- Implementation: `frontend/src/features/views/preprocessing/utils/typeUtils.ts` (new exported `isEmptyValue`, `formatPreviewValue`), `filter/utils/categoricalOptions.ts` (`buildCategoricalOptionEntries`), `filter/components/FilterValueChecklist.tsx`, `filter/hooks/useFilterSubTabSections.tsx`, `utils/autoNodeNames.ts` (`formatScalar`), `views/common/components/RowDetailPanel.tsx` (`formatMetadataValue`), `workspace/data-view/hooks/useWorkspaceDataTable.ts` (`previewSample`), rendered in `DataEditorToolPanel.tsx`.
- Tests: new `preprocessing/utils/__tests__/formatPreviewValue.test.ts`; `categoricalOptions.test.ts` (blank text case); `DataPreprocessingFeature.test.tsx` (hint text).
- v0.8 notes: Keep one user-facing notion of "empty" = null, NaN, or blank/whitespace text everywhere (display, filter labels, generated names). DuckDB distinguishes NULL and NaN (and NaN only exists for float/double); the "is empty" predicate must cover NULL, `isnan()` for floats, and `trim(x) = ''` for text. Empty values sort first in both directions in Concordance Review (that was Polars behaviour, documented now as product behaviour; DuckDB default is NULLS LAST, so set `NULLS FIRST` explicitly if keeping it).

#### V07-070 Data type change: report every unconvertible value, point to the first, with Undo (#183)

- Commits: 688f3d3b, a1eb6f4d
- Area: Data Editor (type change)
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: ANU testers found a type change could silently destroy data (text to integer gave only empty values), because casts use `strict=False`. First version (688f3d3b) warned only when at least 10% of the column was emptied. Chao's follow-up (a1eb6f4d): messy data should not block a conversion, and a few typos in 10,000 rows should not go unnoticed, so the warning now appears whenever any value could not be converted and names the first one.
- What changed:
  - The cast edit endpoint (`POST .../nodes/{node_id}/edits` with `kind: "cast"`) returns response headers: `X-Wordflow-Emptied-Values` (count of values that were not empty before and are empty after), `X-Wordflow-Total-Rows`, and when any were emptied `X-Wordflow-First-Emptied-Row` (1-based) and `X-Wordflow-First-Emptied-Value` (original value cast to text, first 200 characters, URL-encoded). "Empty" uses the same test as Filter's "is empty", so a value already blank is not counted. Non-cast edits send none of these headers. All are added to CORS `expose_headers`.
  - The Data Editor shows a `toast.warning` (15 s) with an "Undo" action that calls `undoNode(nodeId)`:
    - Title, one value: `1 value in "<col>" could not be converted to <type label> and is now empty.`
    - Title, several: `<n> of <rows> values in "<col>" (<pct>%) could not be converted to <type label> and are now empty.` (percentage rounded, minimum 1%, numbers with thousands separators).
    - Description: `The first is row <row> (page <page>): "<value>". Undo restores them.` ("it" for one value). The "(page N)" part is included only when the table is unsorted; page = ceil(row / page_size).
  - No toast when nothing was emptied.
  - Docs (`tutorials/ui.md`): "The conversion never stops because of messy data ..." with the `5OO` example.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - CSV `label` column with values a,b,c,d cast to integer: headers Emptied 4, Total 4, First row 1, First value "a".
  - `year` values `1990`, `about 2000`, blank, `2010` cast to integer: Emptied 1 (blank not counted), First row 2, First value `about%202000`.
  - A rename edit has no `X-Wordflow-Emptied-Values` header.
  - 1,000 counts with `5OO` at row 500 and `9oo` at row 900 cast to integer: Emptied 2, First row 500, value "5OO"; the edit succeeds (status 200) for both integer and float.
  - UI: casting "count" with 2 of 10,000 emptied shows exactly `2 of 10,000 values in "count" (1%) could not be converted to integer and are now empty.`; clicking Undo calls undo on the block.
- Implementation: backend `api/workspaces/nodes.py` `edit_node` (headers), `services/nodes.py` `NodeService.edit_with_report` (new; `edit` now delegates), `EmptiedValuesReport` dataclass, `_emptied_values_report(before, after, column)` (horizontal concat of before/after column with a 1-based row index, then `lost = ~empty(before) & empty(after)`), `services/node_operations.py` `empty_value_expression` (public wrapper of `_empty_value_expression`), `shared/table_transport.py` header constants, `main.py` CORS. Frontend `workspace/common/hooks/useWorkspaceTransformMutations.ts` (`headerCount`, `headerText` with `decodeURIComponent`; cast mutation returns `{node, emptied, rows, firstRow, firstValue}`), `workspace/data-view/hooks/useWorkspaceDataTable.ts` `handleCast`. OpenAPI and generated SDK regenerated.
- Tests: `backend/tests/integration/test_cast_emptied_values.py` (`test_cast_reports_emptied_values`, `test_cast_with_a_few_typos_converts_the_rest`); `useWorkspaceDataTable.test.tsx` ("reports values a type change could not convert, with the first one, and offers Undo").
- v0.8 notes: The report relies on the before and after plans having the same row order so a horizontal concat lines rows up (true for a column cast in Polars). In DuckDB compute it in one query over the source, e.g. `count(*) FILTER (WHERE NOT is_empty(col) AND is_empty(TRY_CAST(col AS T)))` plus the first row number via `row_number()`; use `TRY_CAST` for non-strict casts. Undo depends on v0.7's per-block session plan history; v0.8 needs an equivalent undo for the toast action.

#### V07-074 Data types: Date type added (#187)

- Commits: f701afb4
- Area: Data Editor, Filter, Trends, Export, saved Projects
- Type: feature
- Problem / reason: Many HASS columns are plain dates (publication or sitting dates), but the type menu offered only datetime, which shows a meaningless 00:00 time. Excel dates already arrive as Date (#165).
- What changed:
  - Type menu adds "date" (value `date`). Arrow `Date32<DAY>` columns are labelled "date".
  - Text (String or Categorical) to date opens the same format panel as datetime, titled `Convert “<col>” to date`; parsed with `str.to_date(format=..., strict=...)` or an inferred format. Datetime to date keeps the date (`dt.date()`). Date to date is a no-op. Any other source type is refused: `Column '<col>' is <type>; only text and datetime columns can be converted to date.`
  - Saved Projects: `CastDerivation.target_type` literal adds `"date"`; saved-plan tests cover every type change, including the three date conversions.
  - Trends: accepts Date time columns; hides "second", "minute", "hourly" frequencies and "seconds", "minutes", "hours" custom units when the time column is a Date. If a sub-day period is still requested, the backend casts the Date to `Datetime("us")` (midnight).
  - Filter: for Date columns the frontend sends the local calendar day (`YYYY-MM-DD`) instead of the date picker's UTC timestamp, so midnight in Sydney no longer compares as the previous day; the backend `_parse_temporal` accepts plain `YYYY-MM-DD` values (parsed to a naive datetime at midnight).
  - Export: CSV, Excel, JSON and Parquet write dates as dates (tested).
  - Text to decimal now uses `strict=False`, so unconvertible values become empty (and are reported by #183) instead of refusing the whole change.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Converting "2020-01-31" text to date gives a Date column labelled "date", showing 2020-01-31 with no time.
  - Converting a datetime column to date drops the time.
  - Converting an integer column to date gives the error above.
  - With a Date time column, Trends frequency options exclude second, minute and hourly.
  - Filtering a Date column by a picked day in Australia/Sydney matches rows of that day.
  - Text "1.5, abc" to decimal succeeds with "abc" empty.
  - A Project with a date cast reopens with the cast applied.
- Implementation: backend `services/node_casting.py` (`_date_cast_expr`, `_cast_expr`, `SUPPORTED_CAST_TARGETS`), `domain/workspace/provenance.py` (`CastDerivation`), `services/node_operations.py` (`_cast_is_no_op`, `_DATE_PATTERN`, `_parse_temporal`), `analysis/sequential_core.py` (`_build_sequential_result_frames` sub-day cast). Frontend `workspace/data-view/services/schemaMutations.ts`, `hooks/useColumnMutations.ts` (text to date opens the format panel), `components/WorkspaceTable.tsx` (`targetLabel`), `views/common/components/DatetimeFormatPanel.tsx` (`targetLabel` prop), `lib/arrow/arrowTable.ts` (`isArrowDateField`, display map), `preprocessing/filter/utils/serializers.ts` (`toLocalCalendarDay`, `toCalendarDays`), `sequential-analysis/SequentialAnalysisFeature.tsx` and `panels/SequentialAnalysisParameterPanel.tsx` (`dateOnly`, `SUB_DAY_FREQUENCIES`, `SUB_DAY_UNITS`). OpenAPI regenerated.
- Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_data_editor_saved_plans.py::test_type_change_plan_can_be_read_back`, `test_date_columns_in_tools.py` (`test_trends_accepts_a_date_time_column`, `test_trends_custom_interval_accepts_a_date_column`, `test_date_columns_export_as_dates`), `test_filter_text_values.py::test_filter_compares_date_columns`; frontend `filter/utils/__tests__/serializers.test.ts` ("sends the local calendar day for Date columns", "keeps full timestamps for datetime columns").
- v0.8 notes: Date must be a first-class type (DuckDB DATE). Saved-plan round-trip was a polars-source-utils concern; in v0.8 make sure stored plans or SQL can express date casts (`strptime(...)::DATE`, `TRY_STRPTIME`). The Filter timezone fix is frontend logic (local calendar day for Date columns) and must be kept. Trends with DuckDB: `date_trunc` on DATE for sub-day units needs a cast to TIMESTAMP.

#### V07-080 Column names are exact: whitespace around a name is kept (#108)

- Commits: 9d70ce34
- Area: Analyses (all request models), Data Editor, Frequency (document column), metadata selectors
- Type: bug fix
- Problem / reason: A CSV header "ID, text" names the second column " text" (leading space). The frontend trimmed it before saving the document column (the backend's exact check then rejected it with 400 and a generic toast "Could not save the document column for this data block."), and analysis request models stripped whitespace via `NonEmptyText`, so workers selected a non-existent column (`ColumnNotFoundError: unable to find column "text"`).
- What changed:
  - Backend: new `ColumnName = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(min_length=1)]` (no strip) for every field naming an existing column: `node_columns` (Token Frequency, Topic Modelling, Concordance), Quotation `column`, Trends `time_column` and `group_by_columns`, annotation `text_column`, `annotation_column`, `correction_column`, `class_column`, `description_column`, `example_text_column`, `example_annotation_column`, Data Block creation `selected_columns` and `selected_metadata_columns`, Topic Modelling creation `selected_columns`. `NonEmptyColumnName` in `models/node_resources.py` (used for set_cell and annotation class columns, among others) no longer strips. Free-text fields (names, instructions, search words) still strip.
  - Frontend: `usePersistNodeDocumentColumn` sends the exact column (empty string becomes null); `normalizeMetadataColumns` de-duplicates without trimming.
  - Headers are not normalised on load, so exports keep the source header.
  - Not done (optional polish from the issue): showing leading/trailing spaces visibly in column pickers.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - Loading `ID, text` CSV and choosing " text" as the document column saves without error.
  - Running Frequency on it succeeds.
  - Request models keep " text" and " date " exactly.
  - `normalizeMetadataColumns([' text', 'text'])` keeps both.
- Implementation: `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/domain/workspace/analysis.py`, `backend/src/ldaca_wordflow/models/node_resources.py`, `frontend/src/features/views/common/hooks/usePersistNodeDocumentColumn.ts`, `views/common/components/metadataColumnSelection.ts`.
- Tests: `backend/tests/integration/test_exact_column_names.py` (`test_analysis_requests_keep_whitespace_in_column_names`, `test_document_column_with_a_leading_space_can_be_saved`); `metadataColumnSelection.test.ts`.
- v0.8 notes: In DuckDB, always quote identifiers (`"  text"`), never trim schema names, and do not let CSV readers normalise headers (DuckDB `read_csv` keeps whitespace by default but check `normalize_names` is off). Consider visible whitespace markers in pickers.

#### V07-102 Data Editor layout: title in the tab strip, double-click rename, tab list, type symbols, Switch Project (#206)

- Commits: 3fda1f28
- Area: Data Editor (right column), shared `EditorTabs` (also analysis tabs), Project Graph title bar
- Type: behaviour change / feature
- Problem / reason: requested by Chao: the Data Editor header had a separate title, the Data Block name and a "Rename" button, taking space; type buttons with full type names made columns wide; "integer" is jargon.
- What changed:
  - The "Data Editor" title and its help icon (target `ui.data-viewer`) sit at the left end of the Data Block tab strip. The header's Data Block name label (with RTL clipping fade) and its "Rename" button (aria "Rename node") are removed.
  - The tab strip now shows from one selected Data Block up (before: only with two or more), because it carries the title and renaming. Accessible name of the strip: "Data Block tabs" (old "Selected node tabs").
  - Renaming (shared `EditorTabs`, so both Data Editor and analysis tabs): a single click only activates a tab; renaming starts on double-click of the active tab. Before, a single click on the already active tab started renaming. Keys typed in the rename input no longer reach the tab strip's key handler (`event.target !== event.currentTarget` guard), so Space, arrows, Home and End edit the text.
  - Tab list: a chevron button at the right end of the strip (aria "All Data Block tabs", title "All tabs") opens a dropdown listing all tabs alphabetically (`localeCompare`, base sensitivity, numeric), the active one with a tick; choosing one activates it.
  - Column type button: shows only a symbol plus the chevron. Symbols (`ColumnTypeSymbol`): text "Aa", categorical = tag icon, whole number "123", decimal "1.2", date = calendar icon, date and time = clock icon, true/false = square-check icon, list = list icon, topic coverage = pie chart icon, unknown = circle-help icon. Tooltip (300 ms delay) shows the capitalised type name; the button keeps `aria-label="Change data type for column {column}"` and gets `aria-description` with the type name. The menu lists types with capitalised names. Topic coverage columns keep the disabled-reason tooltip.
  - Type vocabulary: cast option labels "integer" to "whole number", "datetime" to "date and time" (`schemaMutations.ts`); `arrowTypeDisplayName` map `Int64` to "whole number", `Timestamp<MICROSECOND, UTC>` to "date and time", new `Bool` to "true / false". The datetime modal's fallback label "datetime" to "date and time". (Item 4 later renames "categorical" to "category" and maps all remaining types.)
  - Project Graph: "Change Project" to "Switch Project" (busy "Switching…"); help registry label "Switch Project".
  - Docs: ui.md Data Editor section (tabs, double-click rename, tab list, a table of type symbols), Trends tutorial uses "date and time" and "whole number"; screenshots retaken, new `data_editor_tab_list.png`.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - With one Data Block selected, the strip shows "Data Editor", a help icon, one tab and the tab-list arrow.
  - Single click on the active tab does nothing; double-click opens the rename box; typing a space inserts a space.
  - The tab list is alphabetical and ticks the current tab.
  - A text column's type button shows "Aa"; hovering shows "Text"; an Int64 column shows "123" and "Whole number".
  - The Project Graph button reads "Switch Project".
- Implementation: `frontend/src/components/tabs/EditorTabs.tsx` (click/double-click handlers, key guard), `features/workspace/data-view/components/WorkspaceSelectionTabs.tsx`, `ColumnTypeSymbol.tsx` (new), `WorkspaceColumnHeader.tsx` (`field` prop, `typeName`), `WorkspaceDataHeader.tsx` (rename removed), `hooks/useWorkspaceDataTable.ts` (`onTabRename`, `shouldShowTabs = selectedNodeIds.length > 0`), `services/schemaMutations.ts`, `lib/arrow/arrowTable.ts` (`isArrowTimestampField`, `isArrowListField`), `components/layout/ChangeProjectMenu.tsx`.
- Tests: `EditorTabs.test.tsx`, `WorkspaceSelectionTabs.test.tsx` (tab list, rename), `WorkspaceDataHeader.test.tsx` (rename removed), `WorkspaceTable.test.tsx`, `ChangeProjectMenu.test.tsx`, `WorkspaceControls.test.tsx`, `arrowTable.test.ts`, label updates in Trends and Data Builder tests.
- v0.8 notes: symbols come from Arrow field types in v0.7; in v0.8 map DuckDB types (VARCHAR, ENUM, BIGINT, DOUBLE, DATE, TIMESTAMP/TIMESTAMPTZ, BOOLEAN, LIST, STRUCT) to the same user vocabulary and symbols. Keep "activate, then double-click to rename" for all tabs. The rename behaviour itself was later unified by #210.

#### V07-104 Data Editor follow-ups: scrolling tab strip, tools left and Undo/Redo right, wider tab list, steady scroll, double-click column rename (#208)

- Commits: d8e7790d
- Area: Data Editor, shared `EditorTabs`
- Type: bug fix + feature
- Problem / reason: follow-ups from testing #206: the tab strip lost its edge buttons and no longer scrolled; the tab list button was pushed off-screen; the tab list was too narrow; changing a column's type scrolled the table to the top-left; no double-click column rename.
- What changed:
  - `EditorTabs` applies its `className` to the outer box instead of the inner scroller, so a flex parent (`min-w-0 flex-1`) can shrink it; the strip scrolls with its edge buttons again, and the tab list button stays visible at the right end.
  - Toolbar: editing tools (Add column, Find & replace, Clean text, help, Delete columns, and "(empty table)" note) on the left; Undo/Redo pushed to the right end (`ml-auto`); on a narrow panel Undo/Redo wrap to a second line.
  - Tab list width: `w-max min-w-64 max-w-[min(36rem,calc(100vw-2rem))]`, so it sizes to the names up to about 36 characters' width; long names use `MiddleFadeLabel` (keep start and end, fade the middle), full name on hover.
  - Scroll position: `WorkspaceTable` stops using TanStack `flexRender` and calls renderers directly (local `renderColumnPart`), so headers and cells stay mounted when the table re-renders. Before, a type change remounted every cell (1,680 cells and 84 headers in one test case) and WebKit reset the scroll to (0, 0).
  - Double-click a column name to rename it (when renaming is allowed and the column is not busy). Title tooltip "{column} (double-click to rename)"; the rename input has accessible name "Rename column {column}"; Enter submits, Esc cancels.
  - Docs: ui.md Data Editor section and two screenshots updated.
- Expected behaviour (testable):
  - With 7 tabs in a narrow panel, the strip scrolls with arrow buttons and the tab-list arrow stays visible.
  - Undo/Redo are at the right end of the toolbar.
  - After scrolling the table to (300, 150) and changing a column type, the position is still (300, 150) (checked in WebKit).
  - Double-clicking "speaker", typing "member" and Enter calls rename with ("speaker", "member").
- Implementation: `frontend/src/components/tabs/EditorTabs.tsx`, `WorkspaceDataHeader.tsx`, `WorkspaceSelectionTabs.tsx`, `WorkspaceColumnHeader.tsx`, `WorkspaceTable.tsx`. The local `renderColumnPart` was moved to `src/lib/table/renderColumnPart.ts` in #209.
- Tests: `WorkspaceTable.test.tsx`: "renames a column when its name is double-clicked (issue 208)", "keeps cells mounted when the table re-renders, so the scroll position holds (issue 208)" (asserts the same DOM node before and after rerender); `WorkspaceSelectionTabs.test.tsx` updated.
- v0.8 notes: see the #209 entry: column renderers must be plain functions called directly, not mounted as components. WebKit/Safari-specific: remounting a table's cells resets its scroll position.

#### V07-113 Type-change errors in plain words (#205 item 5)

- Commits: d5ec6213
- Area: Data Editor type change (backend `node_casting.py`)
- Type: wording
- Problem / reason: messages were written for programmers ("…Note your notebook example used .head() (sampling)… keep strict=False (default) to set them null.") and pasted raw Polars text.
- What changed (all casting failures end with "Nothing was changed." and carry the Polars text in `details.diagnostic`, shown under Details):
  - Date parse failure: 'Some values in "{column}" don't match the date format {format}. Check the format, or clean those values first. Nothing was changed.' (without a format: "…don't match a date format Wordflow recognises…"). Same wording for sample-validation failures on date/datetime targets.
  - Other sample-validation failure: 'Some values in "{column}" can't be read as {target label}. Clean them first, or choose another type. Nothing was changed.'
  - General cast failure: '"{column}" can't be converted to {target label}. Clean its values first, or choose another type. Nothing was changed.'
  - Wrong source for date: 'Only text and date and time columns can become dates, and "{column}" is neither.' (old "Column 'x' is Int64; only text and datetime columns can be converted to date.")
  - Unsupported target: "Wordflow can't convert columns to {target} yet. Choose text, category, whole number, decimal, date, or date and time."
  - Target labels: string to text, categorical to category, integer to whole number, float to decimal, datetime to date and time, date to date.
- Expected behaviour (testable): converting a column with "31/31/2020" using `%d/%m/%Y` fails with the date sentence and a Details link; the column is unchanged.
